Third, unlike BEV-based methods, our network does not use camera projection parameters, making it robust to camera-extrinsic perturbations. MapLightning uses up to 16.7$\times$ fewer intermediate tokens than dense BEV-based methods and achieves state-of-the-art accuracy and efficiency on nuScenes and Argoverse~2. Its lightweight variant surpasses MapTRv2 by +10.1 mAP on nuScenes and +16.2 mAP on Argoverse~2, while delivering 1.73$\times$ faster inference (40+ FPS) with 53\% less memory. We further show improvements on uncertainty-aware map construction and downstream trajectory prediction. Code and models will be released.

</details>


### [140] [DecomVoxel: Harnessing 3D-Native Priors with Guided In-situ Denoising Optimization for Decompositional Scene Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01914)
*Junfeng Ni,Zirui Zhou,Yixin Chen,Yu Liu,Nan Jiang,Zhifei Yang,Song-Chun Zhu,Siyuan Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DecomVoxel 提出一种引导式原位去噪优化框架，将3D原生先验与神经场景重建结合，用于解决重遮挡下的分解式场景重建，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分解式场景重建旨在重建高质量物体与背景，但现有方法在重遮挡下质量不足。2D图像先验缺乏3D感知导致多视角不一致，而3D原生先验虽具结构归纳偏置，却在复杂场景中引发空间漂移与错位。

Method: 将物体补全建模为引导式原位去噪优化，桥接3D原生先验与神经场景重建；提出重表述的基于epsilon的蒸馏损失以保证潜在表示稳定精修；设计自适应空间引导，利用占据与空闲锚点并配合时间退火，抑制生成幻觉、缓解空间漂移。

Result: 在Replica与ScanNet++上的实验表明，DecomVoxel显著超越当前最优方法，同时忠实保持原始空间布局、结构保真度与风格一致的纹理，并输出拓扑、几何与外观干净的高质量带纹理网格。

Conclusion: DecomVoxel推进了分解式重建的边界，为复杂真实场景的分解式重建提供了鲁棒解决方案，代码已开源。

Abstract: Decompositional scene reconstruction aims to reconstruct high-quality objects and background, yet existing methods still struggle with the level of quality under heavy occlusions. While generative priors offer a potential solution, 2D image-based priors often suffer from multi-view inconsistency due to a lack of 3D awareness. Conversely, 3D-native priors provide stronger structural inductive biases but frequently lead to spatial drift and misalignment within complex scenes. To address these issues, we propose DecomVoxel, formulating object completion as a guided in-situ denoising optimization that bridges 3D-native priors with neural scene reconstruction. Our framework introduces a reformulated epsilon-based distillation loss to ensure stable latent refinement, alongside adaptive spatial guidance that utilizes occupied and vacant anchors with temporal annealing to suppress generative hallucinations and mitigate spatial drift. Experiments on Replica and ScanNet++ show that DecomVoxel significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods while faithfully preserving the original spatial layout, structural fidelity, and style-consistent texture. Our method pushes the boundary of decompositional reconstruction by delivering high-quality textured meshes with clean topology, geometry, and appearance, providing a robust solution for the decompositional reconstruction of complex real-world scenes. Code is available at https://github.com/DecomVoxel/DecomVoxel.

</details>


### [141] [CLoSeR: Closing the Loop for Long-Context Streaming Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01927)
*Moyang Li,Zihan Zhu,Wei Zhang,Marc Pollefeys,Daniel Barath*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 CLoSeR，将回环闭合引入前馈流式重建基础模型，通过全局描述子检索回环候选、构建回环条件窗口估计相对位姿，并在 SE(3) 流形上优化位姿，实现无漂移的千米级重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈基础模型在长上下文流式重建中存在误差累积导致的严重跟踪漂移，需要引入回环闭合来消除漂移、实现千米级精确重建。

Method: 通过全局描述子检索检测回环候选；构建回环条件窗口估计回环帧间相对位姿；利用骨干网络输出全局一致尺度的特性，在 SE(3) 流形上结合顺序约束与回环约束优化所有帧位姿，避免以往在 Sim(3) 或 SL(4) 流形上的位姿图优化。

Result: 大量实验表明该方法在千米级序列上显著降低漂移、生成一致几何，性能大幅超越现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 在流式重建基础模型中重新引入回环闭合，并在 SE(3) 上优化位姿，可有效实现准确、无漂移的千米级流式重建。

Abstract: Feedforward foundation models have recently shown remarkable 3D reconstruction capabilities. However, existing models exhibit large tracking drift in long-context streaming reconstruction due to error accumulation. In this paper, we revisit loop closure with streaming reconstruction foundation models to enable accurate, drift-free, kilometer-scale reconstruction. Specifically, our method detects loop candidates through global descriptor retrieval, and constructs loop-conditioned windows to estimate the relative poses between looped frames. Given the observation that our adopted streaming reconstruction backbone produces a globally consistent scale, we optimize all frame poses on the SE(3) manifold with sequential and loop closure constraints, avoiding the pose graph optimization on the Sim(3) or higher-dimensional SL(4) manifolds employed in prior works. Extensive experiments show that our method reduces drift and produces consistent geometry on kilometer-scale sequences, significantly outperforming the state of the art. Code is available at https://github.com/MoyangLi00/CLoSeR.git.

</details>


### [142] [Fewer Tokens, Better Action: GPT-6 Astra Robot Agents with 14% Higher Success Rate but 65% Fewer Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01939)
*Ruiyang Si,Jianxin Bi,Shunyu Yang,Rui Ni,Wenbo Huang,Qiang Wang,Shulong Jiang,Duomin Wang,Xiuyu Li,Haiwen Feng,Zhen Dong,Daquan Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PyRUA-Lean是一个交互式代码执行框架，将经典机器人原语与VLA策略组合为Python单元，配合选择性观测，在保持较低token开销的同时提升机器人任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM智能体虽可通过视觉反馈和动作原语控制机器人，但重复的模型调用和冗余观测带来了显著的token开销，需要一种更高效的执行与观测机制。

Method: 提出PyRUA-Lean框架，将反馈驱动的原语组合与选择性观测耦合：智能体把经典机器人原语和学到的VLA策略组合进Python单元，执行条件检查和本地重试，仅返回明确请求的图像与状态反馈用于重规划。

Result: 在LIBERO-PRO、RoboTwin 2.0和RoboCasa365的700个模拟任务实例上，与使用相同GPT-6 Astra规划器和底层原语的工具调用基线对比，在相同LLM调用预算下整体成功率从63.1%提升至71.7%；在双方均解决的实例上，LLM调用减少49%，输入token减少65%。

Conclusion: 通过代码执行结合选择性观测，PyRUA-Lean能在降低token与调用开销的同时提高机器人任务成功率，显示出该框架在VLM机器人智能体中的有效性。

Abstract: Vision language model (VLM) agents can control robots through visual feedback and action primitives, but repeated model invocations and redundant observations incur substantial token overhead. We introduce PyRUA-Lean, an interactive code-execution framework that couples feedback-driven primitive composition with selective observation: the agent composes classical robot primitives and learned vision-language-action (VLA) policies into Python cells that perform conditional checks and local retries, returning only explicitly requested images and state feedback for replanning. Across 700 simulated task instances from LIBERO-PRO, RoboTwin 2.0, and RoboCasa365, we compare PyRUA-Lean with a tool-calling baseline using the same GPT-6 Astra planner and underlying robot primitives. Under equal LLM-call budgets, PyRUA-Lean increases overall success from 63.1% to 71.7%. On instances solved by both agents, it uses 49% fewer LLM calls and 65% fewer input tokens.

</details>


### [143] [Latent-Foresight: End-to-End Learning Predictable Representations for Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01942)
*Efstathios Karypidis,Spyros Gidaris,Nikos Komodakis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Latent-Foresight，端到端联合学习潜在分词器与流式生成动力学模型，构建面向时间可预测性的潜在空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界建模需预测场景未来演化。现有方法依赖两阶段流程：先用固定降维（如PCA）或独立自编码器压缩VFM特征，再在冻结潜在空间上训练预测器。表征学习与时序预测解耦，或直接在原始VFM特征上做预测，都无法保证潜在空间适合可预测的动态。

Method: 端到端框架，联合学习潜在分词器（latent tokenizer）与基于流的生成动力学模型（flow-based generative dynamics model），显式塑造表征以支持时间可预测性。引入关键设计选择以防止潜在塌缩，并使重建目标与生成目标对齐，从而实现稳定联合优化。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法学习到时间上更连贯的潜在表征，在多种未来场景理解任务和预测时域上持续优于两阶段基线，同时省去独立训练阶段（包括高分辨率适配阶段）。代码与权重已开源。

Conclusion: 联合优化表征与动力学预测可有效提升未来场景理解的表征质量与预测性能，消除两阶段流程的冗余，为世界建模提供更优的潜在空间设计范式。

Abstract: Predicting the future evolution of a scene is a fundamental capability for world modeling. Recent work has shown that operating in the feature space of Vision Foundation Models (VFMs) yields semantically rich representations that support diverse future scene understanding tasks. However, existing approaches rely on two-stage pipelines, where VFM features are first compressed using fixed dimensionality reduction (e.g., PCA) or independently trained autoencoders, and a separate predictor is trained on top of the resulting frozen latent space. This decoupling between representation learning and temporal prediction, as well as approaches that apply predictors directly on raw VFM features, provides no guarantee that the latent space is structured for predictable dynamics. In this work, we propose Latent-Foresight, an end-to-end framework that jointly learns a latent tokenizer and a flow-based generative dynamics model, explicitly shaping the representation to support temporal predictability. To enable stable joint optimization, we introduce several key design choices that prevent latent collapse and align reconstruction with generative objectives. Extensive experiments show that our approach learns more temporally coherent latent representations and consistently outperforms two-stage baselines across multiple future scene understanding tasks and prediction horizons, while eliminating separate training stages, including during high-resolution adaptation. We provide the implementation code and model weights at https://github.com/Sta8is/Latent-Foresight

</details>


### [144] [Anti-Persona: Disrupting Unauthorized Identity Binding and Recognition in Personalized Vision--Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01944)
*Abhishek Basu,Fahad Shamshad,Karthik Nandakumar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Anti-Persona，一种针对个性化LVLM的图像级防御方法，通过破坏身份原型对齐来阻止未授权身份绑定与识别，保护率达95%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少样本个性化让LVLM学习用户特定视觉概念，但也带来隐私风险：攻击者可用少量参考图绑定目标身份，并通过自然语言查询在新图中识别该身份，因此需要一种防止未授权身份绑定与识别的防御手段。

Method: 核心洞察是身份个性化依赖多张参考图共享的视觉特征。方法将这些特征聚合为身份原型，在视觉编码器空间中优化视觉上细微的扰动以破坏原型对齐；同时采用空间平滑与低频保留来提升视觉保真度和抗图像压缩的实用性。该保护不依赖特定提示，支持主动式反个性化与被动式图像保护。

Result: 在两个代表性个性化LVLM上，保护率最高达95.0%，同时保持视觉保真度；方法在提示变化和所评估的身份查询任务中保持稳定，并在编码器不匹配情况下提升了黑盒迁移性。

Conclusion: Anti-Persona能有效防止个性化LVLM中的未授权身份绑定与识别，兼具高保护率、视觉保真度与良好的鲁棒性和迁移性。

Abstract: Few-shot personalization enables large vision--language models (LVLMs) to learn user-specific visual concepts for applications such as personalized retrieval and subject-aware querying. However, it also creates a privacy risk: an adversary can bind a target identity from a few reference images and subsequently detect that identity in new images through natural-language queries. We introduce Anti-Persona, an image-level defense against unauthorized identity binding and recognition in personalized LVLMs. Our key insight is that identity personalization relies on visual features shared across multiple reference images. We aggregate these features into an identity prototype and optimize visually subtle perturbations that disrupt prototype alignment in the vision-encoder space. Spatial smoothing and low-frequency preservation further promote visual fidelity and practical resilience to image compression. The resulting protection does not depend on a specific prompt and supports both proactive anti-personalization and reactive image protection. Experiments on two representative personalized LVLMs demonstrate protection rates of up to $95.0\%$ while preserving visual fidelity. The method remains stable across prompt variations and evaluated identity-query tasks, and improves black-box transfer under encoder mismatch.

</details>


### [145] [EndoLive: Real-Time Style Transfer for Endoscopic Endonasal Skull Base Surgical Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01956)
*Griffin Hurt,Calvin Brinkman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 EndoLive 框架，实现尸体内窥镜视频与活体人内窥镜视频之间的实时风格迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内镜下颅底手术等复杂手术需要大量训练，通常使用尸体标本。但尸体组织经防腐处理，颜色与活体差异明显，且无法像活体那样出血，导致关键解剖结构的识别在尸体练习与真实手术中差别较大。

Method: 结合 ConStructS GAN（用于手术场景的逼真风格迁移）与 HyPER-GAN（可学习复杂转换并实时执行），在非配对的尸体与活体内窥镜图像上进行训练，并在多种设备上用尸体视频测试。

Result: EndoLive 能以远高于实时最低要求的速度完成尸体到活体的转换，同时保持关键解剖结构的语义一致性。源代码已开源。

Conclusion: 该框架有望提升尸体训练与真实手术之间视觉一致性，为手术训练提供更接近活体的视觉体验。

Abstract: Complex surgical procedures around critical anatomy, such as the endoscopic endonasal skull base surgery, requires significant practice and training on the part of the surgeon before they are allowed to perform the operation on a live patient. This training in typically done in cadaveric specimens, due to them containing the same critical structures as a living human. However, cadavers are not a perfect 1-to-1 substitute for a living patient. The dead and preserved tissues of a cadaver are colored completely differently than a living human, and -- without complex and expensive pumping systems -- do not bleed in the same way. As a result, identifying the critical pieces of anatomy that make this procedure so complex can be quite different in a live case than in a surgeon's cadaveric practice. This paper presents EndoLive, a framework for real-time style transfer between cadaveric endoscopic video and living human endoscopic video. Our method combines the ConStructS GAN model for realistic style transfer for surgical applications, with the HyPER-GAN model that can learn complex translations and perform them in real-time. We train EndoLive on unpaired cadaveric and live images taken from an endoscope, and test the trained model with cadaveric video, on a variety of devices. Experimental results demonstrate that EndoLive can perform cadaveric-to-live translation at speeds well above the minimum necessary for real-time, while maintaining semantic consistency of critical anatomical structures. Our source code is available at https://github.com/griffhurt/endolive.

</details>


### [146] [RASteer: Retain-Aware Activation Steering for Concept Erasure in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01969)
*Yongliang Wu,Haori Lu,Yulun Wu,Jinqi Luo,Xingyu Zhu,Yaoyao Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练方法RASteer，通过构建保留子空间并移除擦除方向中的共享分量，结合重叠自适应校准，实现概念擦除与内容保留的更好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有激活引导方法基于目标概念构建擦除方向，在推理时沿该方向调整激活；但目标与保留概念在表示空间中重叠，擦除方向含保留概念依赖的共享分量，直接引导会抑制保留概念、损害非目标内容生成。

Method: RASteer为免训练方法：先由待保留概念构建保留子空间；Retain-Orthogonal Steering（ROS）从擦除方向中移除与该子空间对齐的分量，使引导更针对目标；再引入Overlap-Adaptive Calibration（OAC），在每个层与去噪步依据擦除方向与保留子空间的重叠度控制共享分量移除比例，以平衡擦除与保留。

Result: 在多种骨干网络与基准上，针对不安全内容、实例与艺术风格擦除的实验表明，RASteer达到或超过所评估的激活引导与权重编辑基线，在擦除与保留之间取得更佳平衡。

Conclusion: RASteer通过正交化与自适应校准有效缓解激活引导中的共享分量干扰，无需训练即可在保持非目标内容生成能力的同时实现目标概念擦除。

Abstract: Concept erasure aims to remove a target concept, such as a copyrighted style, a recognizable character, or unsafe content, from a pretrained text-to-image diffusion model while preserving its ability to generate other content. Existing activation steering methods build an erasure direction mainly from the target concept and adjust model activations along it at inference time. However, target and retained concepts often overlap in the model's representation space, so this direction also contains shared components that retained concepts rely on. Steering directly along this direction can therefore suppress retained concepts and harm the generation of non-target content. To address this issue, we propose Retain-aware Activation Steering (RASteer), a training-free method. RASteer first builds a retain subspace from the concepts to preserve. Retain-Orthogonal Steering (ROS) then removes components aligned with this subspace from the erasure direction, making steering more specific to the target. Since fully removing the shared components can weaken erasure, we further introduce Overlap-Adaptive Calibration (OAC). At each layer and denoising step, OAC uses the overlap between the erasure direction and the retain subspace to control how much of each shared component is removed, balancing target erasure and concept preservation. Experiments on unsafe-content, instance, and artistic-style erasure across multiple backbones and benchmarks show that RASteer matches or outperforms the activation steering and weight editing baselines we evaluate, achieving a better balance between erasure and preservation.

</details>


### [147] [Token-Level Video Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01973)
*Yifan Wang,Gordon Guocheng Qian,Yanyu Li,Anil Kag,Yun Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Token级视频强化学习框架TVRL，利用冻结视觉语言模型的答案似然及其对视频输入的梯度，推导出token级信用，用于重加权去噪转移对数概率，在VBench-2.0上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统视频生成强化学习对整个采样视频分配单一标量奖励，但视频中各视觉token的缺陷并不均匀：部分token已满足提示，部分仍需修正。标量奖励无法定位误差，导致优化扰动了本已良好的token，而对真正需要改变的token关注不足。

Method: 提出TVRL框架，从被优化的奖励中推导token级信用。核心思路是：冻结视觉语言模型的答案似然既可提供视频级奖励信号，其视频输入梯度的幅值又能揭示哪些生成token对该分数影响最大。在GRPO中实例化：将提示衍生的问题奖励平均为组相对优势，并用分离梯度、以问题为条件的token信用图，在裁剪策略比内重加权密集去噪转移的对数概率。

Result: 在VBench-2.0上，TVRL取得Overall 57.69分，较基座模型提升3.60分；在SAGE、Flow、Dance三种SDE采样器上较匹配的GRPO基线提升2.68--3.15分；在VideoAlign、VideoScore2、UnifiedReward2、Qwen3.5-9B四种奖励模型上提升1.33--3.15分。

Conclusion: TVRL通过从奖励中提取token级信用，实现了视频生成强化学习中更精细的误差定位与优化，在多种采样器和奖励模型下均稳定超越标量奖励基线。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) for video generation usually assigns one scalar reward to an entire sampled video. Yet a video is not uniformly flawed: some visual tokens may already satisfy the prompt, whereas others require correction. A scalar reward cannot localize errors, causing optimization to perturb satisfactory tokens while under-targeting the tokens that actually need to change. We introduce Token-Level Video Reinforcement Learning, TVRL, a framework that derives token-level credit from the reward being optimized. Our key insight is that the answer likelihood of a frozen vision-language model provides both signals: its outputs contribute to the video-level reward, while magnitudes of its video-input gradients reveal which generated video tokens most affect that score. We instantiate TVRL in Group Relative Policy Optimization by averaging prompt-derived question rewards into one group-relative advantage and using detached, question-conditioned token-credit maps to reweight dense denoising-transition log-probabilities inside the clipped policy ratio. On VBench-2.0, TVRL achieves an Overall score of 57.69, outperforming the base model by 3.60 points. TVRL also improves matched GRPO baselines across three SDE samplers (SAGE, Flow, and Dance) by 2.68--3.15 points and across four reward models (VideoAlign, VideoScore2, UnifiedReward2, and Qwen3.5-9B) by 1.33--3.15 points.

</details>


### [148] [Continual Concept Erasure in Diffusion Models by Suppressing Cross-Edit Interference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01989)
*Yongliang Wu,Haori Lu,Jinqi Luo,Wei Cao,Xingyu Zhu,Yaoyao Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CEASE是一种免训练的概念擦除方法，通过自适应子空间编辑解决持续擦除中的干扰累积问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着擦除请求随时间不断到来，模型需要在移除新目标的同时不破坏此前的擦除效果。现有方法未能约束跨编辑的干扰：保留集之外的残差扰动会相互作用并累积，导致无关生成质量下降，甚至使先前擦除的目标坍缩为噪声。因此需要一种能抑制干扰累积的持续擦除方案。

Method: 提出CEASE（Continual Erasure via Adaptive Subspace Editing），一种免训练方法，在闭式求解器上施加两个子空间约束：1）将共享替换token表示加入求解器的不变性矩阵；2）当检测到干扰时，将当前更新投影到从累积历史更新中提取的主导输出方向的正交补空间上。并通过闭式分解将累积干扰归因于共享替换的重复激活与连续更新方向的重叠。

Result: 在名人、艺术风格和实例的持续擦除任务上，CEASE实现了最一致的擦除-保留权衡；而现有方法要么损害通用生成能力，要么擦除目标不充分。

Conclusion: CEASE通过两个子空间约束分别抑制共享替换重复激活与更新方向重叠这两类干扰来源，为持续概念擦除提供了一种有效且无需训练的解决方案。

Abstract: Concept erasure removes copyright-protected, privacy-sensitive, or otherwise undesirable concepts from pretrained text-to-image diffusion models to support content governance and compliance. As erasure requests arrive over time, models must remove new targets without undoing prior erasures. Existing methods do not constrain interference across edits: residual perturbations outside the retain set interact and accumulate, degrading unrelated generations and sometimes collapsing previously erased targets into noise. We propose CEASE (Continual Erasure via Adaptive Subspace Editing), a training-free method that imposes two subspace constraints on a closed-form solver. CEASE adds the token representation of the shared replacement to the solver's invariance matrix and, when interference is detected, projects the current update onto the orthogonal complement of dominant output directions extracted from cumulative past updates. A closed-form decomposition attributes the accumulated interference to repeated activation of the shared replacement and overlap between successive update directions, showing that the two constraints suppress these respective sources. Across continual erasure of celebrities, artistic styles, and instances, CEASE achieves the most consistent erase-preserve trade-off, while existing methods either degrade general generation or insufficiently erase targets.

</details>


### [149] [Comparing a gradient boosting algorithm to the GOES FDC for wildfire detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01994)
*Asaf Vanunu,Boaz Nadler,Arnon Karnieli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出基于GOES ABI影像与CatBoost的野火检测方法，在精度、误报和时效性上均优于业务化GOES FDC产品。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 野火对人类生命、生态系统和财产构成严重威胁，而现有地球静止轨道火灾产品（如GOES FDC）在准确性和夜间检测能力上存在不足，需要更优的检测手段。

Method: 使用数千张ABI影像和超过30万条匹配的VIIRS火灾探测数据训练CatBoost模型，并在覆盖五个区域的独立数据集上评估，与业务化GOES FDC产品对比。

Result: CatBoost在训练区内外均取得更高的精确率、召回率和F1分数，F1比GOES FDC高0.16至0.38；在51起历史火灾事件中，CatBoost提前探测到26起，而GOES FDC仅提前探测6起；夜间检测中CatBoost表现准确，而GOES FDC召回率仅约0.03。

Conclusion: 机器学习模型相较现有地球静止轨道火灾产品具有显著改进，包括更高精度、更少误报和更早探测，展现出应用潜力。

Abstract: Wildfires pose severe risks to human life, ecosystems, and property. This study presents a machine learning approach for wildfire detection from GOES ABI imagery. A CatBoost model was trained on a large dataset with thousands of ABI images and over 300,000 matching VIIRS fire detections. An evaluation on a separate dataset across five regions showed that the learned CatBoost model outperformed the operational GOES Fire Detection and Characterization (FDC) product. It achieved higher precision, recall, and F1 scores both within and outside the training area. The CatBoost model achieved F1 scores that were 0.16 to 0.38 higher than the GOES FDC in all regions. In addition, out of 51 historical fire events, the CatBoost detected 26 fires before both VIIRS and GOES FDC, compared to only six earlier detections by the GOES FDC. Importantly, the CatBoost model achieved accurate wildfire detection also during nighttime, whereas the GOES FDC obtained very low recall values, around 0.03. This study demonstrates that machine learning models may offer significant improvements over existing geostationary fire products, including higher accuracy, fewer false alarms, and earlier detection.

</details>


### [150] [From Reasoning Failures to Composable Video Spatial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01999)
*Pengzhan Sun,Junbin Xiao,Ramanathan Rajaraman,Shiu-hong Kao,Angela Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文拆解视觉语言模型空间推理的能力维度，诊断四类常见错误来源，并提出免训练的几何算子库 CROSS，在五个基准上提升空间推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任务级分数无法揭示模型在空间推理中成功或失败的具体能力原因，需要将空间推理拆解为可诊断的能力维度以定位系统性错误。

Method: 在统一模式与坐标约定下比较预测与真值空间上下文，识别四类错误：感知不准、空间上下文信息缺失、测量选择错误、参考系或位置朝向跟踪错误；据此构建 CROSS——免训练的带类型几何算子与空间技能库，为 VLM 提供验证过的上下文或可调用技能。

Result: 在五个基准上评估 CROSS：ReVSI 平均分从 55.9% 提升至 60.2%，DSI-Bench 上 SpatialClaw 从 62.8% 提升至 66.3%。

Conclusion: 显式处理空间约定可在无需额外训练的情况下修复系统性推理失败，验证了能力拆解诊断与算子库设计的有效性。

Abstract: Spatial reasoning benchmarks evaluate vision-language models across diverse tasks, but task-level scores do not reveal which underlying capabilities account for success or failure. Each task requires recovering spatial evidence, representing geometry, and reasoning over it. We disentangle these capabilities by comparing predicted and ground-truth spatial context under a shared schema and coordinate contract. This comparison reveals four recurring sources of error: inaccurate perception, missing information in the spatial context, selection of the wrong measurement, and errors in reference frames or in tracking position and orientation. Guided by this diagnosis, we develop CROSS, a training-free library of typed geometric operators and spatial skills that function over available evidence to support reliable video spatial reasoning. The resulting library supplies verified context to non-coding VLMs or callable skills to a SpatialClaw agent. We evaluate \methodname{} on five benchmarks. \methodname{} raises the average score from 55.9\% to 60.2\% on ReVSI and improves the SpatialClaw result from 62.8\% to 66.3\% on DSI-Bench. These gains demonstrate that explicit handling of spatial conventions can repair systematic reasoning failures without additional training.

</details>


### [151] [Weather-Aware Domain Adaptation for Street-View Weather Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02000)
*Hossein Maghsoumi,George Atia,Yaser P. Fallah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出WA-ADDA方法，通过让域判别器以预测天气为条件，实现域不变且天气敏感的特征，从而在域偏移下提升街景图像多类天气识别，并构建了标准化基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 雨、雪、雾、尘等恶劣天气对自动驾驶中基于摄像头的感知仍是难题。现有天气识别训练数据多来自非街景来源，与真实驾驶场景差异显著，导致域偏移问题。

Method: 提出Weather-Aware Adversarial Discriminative Domain Adaptation (WA-ADDA)，使域判别器以预测天气为条件，促进域不变且天气敏感的特征。同时整合多源非街景天气数据集为源域、真实街景图像为目标域，构建统一基准，并以宏平均准确率为主指标制定标准化评估协议。

Result: 在ResNet-50、EfficientNet、VGG、DenseNet等主干网络上，WA-ADDA持续提升街景识别性能，在恶劣天气下取得较高的各类召回率，同时保持晴天准确率。

Conclusion: 研究证明了域自适应天气识别的可行性，并说明该基准对推进鲁棒的车载感知具有价值。

Abstract: Adverse conditions such as rain, snow, fog, and dust remain challenging for camera-based perception in autonomous driving. We study multi-class weather recognition from street-view images under domain shift, where most available training data come from non-street-view sources that differ markedly from real driving scenes. We propose Weather-Aware Adversarial Discriminative Domain Adaptation (WA-ADDA), which conditions the domain discriminator on predicted weather to promote features that are both domain-invariant and weather-sensitive. We also assemble a multi-dataset benchmark by unifying diverse non-street-view weather collections as sources and real street-view images as targets, and define a standardized evaluation protocol with macro accuracy as the primary metric. Across backbones (ResNet-50, EfficientNet, VGG, DenseNet), WA-ADDA consistently improves street-view performance and yields strong per-class recalls in challenging conditions while preserving clear-weather accuracy. These findings highlight the feasibility of domain-adapted weather recognition and the value of our benchmark for advancing robust, on-board perception.

</details>


### [152] [Exploring Weaknesses of Generative Image Watermarks against Latent Frequency Masking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02010)
*Kirill Aistov,Khaled Abud,Irina Serzhenko,Egor Kovalev,Aleksey Yakushev,Aleksandr Akimenkov,Dmitry Obydenkov,Yury Markin,Sergey Lavrushkin,Dmitriy Vatolin,Anastasia Antsiferova*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出潜在频率掩蔽（Latent Frequency Masking）攻击，通过在潜在表示的傅里叶域替换选定系数来擦除水印证据，并在六种扩散水印方法上验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI生成图像的无形水印是溯源的核心工具，但其对抗自适应移除攻击的鲁棒性仍是未解的安全问题。

Method: 提出潜在频率掩蔽攻击：在含水印图像的潜在表示中替换选定的傅里叶系数，替换值可用高斯噪声采样（高效）或由扩散再生得到（图像保真更好）；并给出关联重建对抗图像与潜在频率扰动变化的理论失真界。

Result: 在DiffusionDB与MS-COCO提示生成的图像上，针对六种扩散水印方法进行评估，该攻击能移除或显著削弱多种水印，同时保持感知质量，且运行时间优于现有攻击。

Conclusion: 潜在频率操纵是一种实用的攻击面，生成式图像水印的鲁棒性评估中应纳入此类攻击。

Abstract: Invisible watermarking has become a central tool for tracing AI-generated images, but its robustness against adaptive removal attacks remains an open security question. We introduce Latent Frequency Masking, an attack that erases watermark evidence by replacing selected Fourier coefficients in the latent representation of a watermarked image. The replacement can be sampled from Gaussian noise for efficiency or derived from diffusion regeneration for improved image preservation. We provide a theoretical distortion bound relating the change between the reconstructed adversarial image and the masked latent-frequency perturbation. We evaluate the proposed attack against six diffusion watermarking methods on images generated from DiffusionDB and MS-COCO prompts. Latent Frequency Masking removes or substantially weakens several watermarks while preserving perceptual quality and achieving favorable runtime compared with existing attacks. These results identify latent-frequency manipulation as a practical attack surface and highlight the need to include such attacks in robustness evaluations of generative image watermarking.

</details>


### [153] [Task-Adaptive Grounded 3D-Programmers Using 2D VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02021)
*Arman Raayatsanati,Sombit Dey,Anna-Maria Halacheva,Jan-Nico Zaech,Luc Van Gool,Danda Pani Paudel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Canonical Coordinate Framing (CCF) 与 Task-Adaptive Feedback (TAF)，让 2D 视觉语言模型无需重训练即可可靠地完成 3D 任务，并构建了 3D-Prog 框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型（VLM）虽在 2D 泛化与推理上表现优异，但 3D 理解受限于数据规模、训练多样性和推理能力。作者不主张简单地将模型扩展到 3D，而是希望让强大的 2D VLM 在 3D 中可靠运行。

Method: 引入两个新概念：Canonical Coordinate Framing (CCF) 作为统一视觉表示，将输入输出锚定到共享欧氏坐标系，解决 3D grounding 中的轴歧义、度量尺度不一致和浮动参考等问题；Task-Adaptive Feedback (TAF) 通过任务自适应的动态反馈闭合推理回路，使 2D VLM 能在原生视觉上下文中执行多种开放词汇任务。基于此构建 3D-Prog 框架，联合使用 CCF、TAF 与 VLM，无需重训练即可完成 3D 理解、操作与生成。

Result: 3D-Prog 在对象级和场景级任务上实现了开放词汇的 3D 理解、操作和生成。实验表明，CCF 与 TAF 的联合使用将 2D VLM 转化为具备几何感知能力的 3D 编程器，在多样 3D 任务中取得一致、可解释且高质量的结果。

Conclusion: 通过 CCF 与 TAF，无需重训练即可让 2D VLM 胜任 3D 任务，为 3D 理解与生成提供了新范式，并展现出良好的泛化性与可解释性。

Abstract: Recent vision-language models (VLMs) exhibit remarkable generalization and reasoning abilities, yet 3D understanding in these models is limited by data scale, training diversity, and reasoning capacity. Instead of naively extending these models into 3D, we take a different approach: we enable powerful 2D VLMs to operate reliably in 3D by introducing 3D grounding and iterative feedback loops with two novel concepts: Canonical Coordinate Framing (CCF) and Task-Adaptive Feedback (TAF). CCF serves as a unified visual representation that anchors both inputs and outputs to a shared Euclidean coordinate system, solving common challenges in 3D grounding such as axis ambiguity, inconsistent metric scale, and floating references. Complementary to this structured framing of the 3D inputs, TAF closes the reasoning loop with task-adaptive dynamic feedback that enables 2D VLMs to perform varied open-vocabulary tasks within their native visual context.
  Building on this foundation, we introduce 3D-Prog, a 3D understanding, reasoning, and generation framework that jointly employs the capabilities of CCF and TAF together with powerful VLMs. Without requiring any retraining, 3D-Prog performs open-vocabulary 3D understanding, manipulation, and generation across both object-level and scene-level tasks. Our experiments show that the joint use of CCF and TAF transforms 2D VLMs into geometry-aware 3D programmers, achieving consistent, interpretable, and high-quality results across diverse 3D tasks.

</details>


### [154] [DiDE:Direct Injection with Color-Texture DEcoupling for 3D Stylization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02044)
*Tao Wu,Alexandra Gomez-Villa,Senmao Li,Yaxing Wang,Joost van de Weijer,Kai Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 DiDE，首个面向解耦式 3D 风格化（Disen3D）的免训练框架，通过通道划分在结构化隐空间中独立编码纹理与颜色，实现无干扰的多参考风格迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于整流流的图生3D生成模型虽具备强3D先验，可用于免训练风格化，但其风格迁移是"全有或全无"：颜色与纹理被联合迁移，无法独立控制，作者将此局限形式化为 Disentangled 3D Stylization(Disen3D)。

Method: 核心观察是图生3D模型的结构化隐空间相对于纹理而言是过完备的：纹理信息仅占据风格显著通道的一小部分，剩余自由子空间可用于独立编码颜色。DiDE 据此设计通道划分机制，分别用内容图、纹理参考、颜色参考经专用分支处理，并在每个自注意力层无干扰地合成两种风格信号，同时保持内容几何。

Result: 在作者新建的多参考基准 Disen3D-Bench 上，DiDE 在颜色保真度、纹理迁移和内容保持三方面持续优于 2D 与 3D 风格化基线。

Conclusion: DiDE 作为首个免训练 Disen3D 框架，验证了利用隐空间通道解耦实现颜色与纹理独立控制的可行性，为可控 3D 风格化提供了新思路。

Abstract: Recent advances in rectified flow-based image-to-3D generative models have enabled high-fidelity 3D asset generation. Building on this, a growing line of work has exploited these strong 3D priors for training-free stylization, transferring visual attributes from a reference image onto a generated 3D asset. However, existing methods enforce an all-or-nothing paradigm: color and texture are transferred jointly, with no mechanism to control them independently -- a limitation we formalize as Disentangled 3D Stylization(Disen3D). To address this, we propose DiDE, the first training-free framework for Disen3D. Key to our approach is the observation that the structured latent space of image-to-3D models is overcomplete with respect to texture: texture information occupies only a small subset of the style-significant channels, leaving a free subspace available for independent color encoding. DiDE exploits this via a channel partition mechanism that processes a content image, a texture reference, and a color reference through dedicated branches and composes both style signals interference-free at every self-attention layer, preserving content geometry throughout. Experiments on Disen3D-Bench, our newly collected multi-reference benchmark, show that DiDE consistently outperforms 2D and 3D stylization baselines in color fidelity, texture transfer, and content preservation.

</details>


### [155] [Form and Void: Entangled Composition through an Autonomous AI Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02045)
*Shiwen Wang,Jian Yang,Xu Wang,Xincan Wang,Weiming Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 FaV-A（Form and Void Agent），一个用于分阶段生成正负空间构图的 multimodal agent，通过先画主体、再分析负空间语义、最后生成构图指令的工作流，显著优于直接单次提示的零样本 MLLM 基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正负空间是视觉构图的基本原则，但生成这类构图颇具挑战，因为需要协调控制两个共享同一边界的语义概念。尽管现有文生图模型与多模态大模型在图像生成和视觉理解上表现强劲，正负空间生成仍然困难，尤其是在直接单次提示（single-pass prompting）条件下。因此作者希望构建一个更有效的框架来完成此任务。

Method: 提出 FaV-A（Form and Void Agent），一个面向分阶段正负空间生成的多模态智能体。它遵循渐进式工作流：首先生成一个基础物体（base object）；然后分析其形状与空间结构，识别候选的负空间语义；最后生成构图指令（compositional instructions），用于最终图像生成阶段。

Result: 实验结果表明，FaV-A 相比直接的零样本 MLLM 基线，能生成视觉上更连贯、语义上更对齐的正负空间构图；消融分析也支持这一结论。

Conclusion: FaV-A 提供了一个比直接零样本 MLLM 基线更有效的框架，用于生成视觉连贯且语义对齐的正负空间构图，验证了分阶段、多模态智能体式方法在此类协调控制任务中的优势。

Abstract: Positive and negative space is a fundamental principle in visual composition, supporting visually coherent forms and layered semantic relationships. Generating such compositions is challenging because it requires coordinated control over two semantic concepts that share a common boundary. Although recent text-to-image models and multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have achieved strong performance in image generation and visual understanding, positive-negative space generation remains difficult, particularly under direct single-pass prompting. In this work, we present the \textbf{F}orm \textbf{a}nd \textbf{V}oid \textbf{A}gent (\textbf{FaV-A}), a multimodal agent designed for staged positive-negative space generation. FaV-A follows a progressive workflow: it first generates a base object, then analyzes its shape and spatial structure to identify candidate negative-space semantics, and finally produces compositional instructions for the final image generation stage. Experimental results and ablation analyses suggest that FaV-A provides a more effective framework than direct zero-shot MLLM baselines for producing visually coherent and semantically aligned positive-negative space compositions.

</details>


### [156] [Learning from Failure: Leveraging Unreliable Predictions in Semi-Supervised Real-World Adverse Weather Removal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02051)
*Cap Dang Xuan Kiet,Tat-Jen Cham*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出学生-教师半监督框架，配合不可靠/可靠数据库对比学习与相位谱语义约束，提升恶劣天气图像复原的真实场景泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一复原模型依赖合成监督、语义约束不足，导致对真实场景泛化能力有限。

Method: 学生-教师半监督框架：构建不可靠数据库保存失败的教师预测作为负样本，可靠数据库保存高质量预测作为正样本，用于对比学习；提出基于相位谱的语义约束替代昂贵的文本监督；设计自适应相位一致性损失，按退化严重程度动态平衡输入与教师伪真值间的监督。

Result: 在真实世界基准上，复原质量与感知保真度持续优于现有最先进方法，并对真实恶劣天气具有更强泛化性。

Conclusion: 联合利用可靠伪真值与不可靠教师输出，并引入高效相位语义先验，可有效提升恶劣天气图像复原的真实场景鲁棒性。

Abstract: Adverse weather image restoration aims to recover images degraded by rain, haze, snow, and other weather-induced artifacts, thereby improving the robustness of outdoor vision systems. Existing unified restoration models exhibit limited generalization to real-world scenes due to their reliance on synthetic supervision and insufficient semantic constraints. In this paper, we propose a novel student--teacher semi-supervised framework that addresses both challenges. Specifically, we introduce an unreliable database that preserves failed teacher predictions as informative negative samples for contrastive learning, while a reliable database stores high-quality teacher predictions as positive samples. By jointly exploiting reliable pseudo-ground truths and unreliable teacher outputs, the proposed framework learns to enhance desirable restoration characteristics while avoiding common failures. We further propose a phase spectrum-based semantic constraint that replaces computationally expensive text-based supervision with an efficient and naturally aligned semantic prior. An adaptive phase consistency loss is also designed to dynamically balance supervision between the degraded input and teacher pseudo-ground truths according to degradation severity. Extensive experiments on real-world benchmarks demonstrate that the proposed method consistently outperforms existing state-of-the-art approaches in restoration quality and perceptual fidelity while exhibiting stronger generalization to real-world adverse weather conditions.

</details>


### [157] [GeoLatent: Geometry-Guided Latent Structuring with Routed Optimization for 3D Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02091)
*Yakun Zhu,Yi Bin,Yujuan Ding,Zheng Wang,Pengpeng Zeng,Duo Peng,Jingkuan Song,Heng Tao Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GeoLatent，通过CR-GEO与路由优化分解并结构化空间几何隐变量，强化隐变量中介的答案学习，显著提升3D空间推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从2D图像进行3D空间推理仍具挑战：文本方法用离散token描述几何，难以刻画连续空间关系；单一连续隐变量类型无法显式分离不同空间任务所需线索；分解式空间隐变量虽分离位置、方向与全局几何，但几何表示易塌缩到单一主导方向，且无约束注意力会使隐变量在答案学习中被欠利用。

Method: GeoLatent结合Common-Residual Geometry Alignment（CR-GEO）与路由优化：CR-GEO将共享几何与残差教师几何分离；路由优化联合训练几何与语言，临时让视觉答案学习经由隐变量进行，随后恢复完整注意力并施加几何监督。

Result: 受控对比中，CR-GEO将几何有效秩从1.00提升至3.87；在瓶颈处阻断隐变量读出时，128个固定问题的方向准确率从89.1%降至25.8%。恢复后，差异化几何表示与隐变量中介的视觉路径仍与直接图像访问并存。GeoLatent在SPAR-Bench达73.0%，SPBench达72.1%，均优于此前方法。

Conclusion: GeoLatent通过结构化分解几何隐变量与路由优化，缓解几何塌缩和隐变量欠利用问题，在多个空间推理基准上取得领先表现。

Abstract: Despite progress in vision-language models, 3D spatial reasoning from 2D images remains challenging. Text-based methods describe intermediate geometry with discrete tokens, limiting fidelity for continuous spatial relations. Continuous latents offer richer representations, but a single latent type does not explicitly separate the cues needed across spatial tasks. Decomposed spatial latents address this by representing position, direction, and global geometry separately under geometric supervision. Yet the geometry representation can still collapse toward one dominant direction, and unrestricted attention can leave the latents underused during answer learning. We introduce GeoLatent, combining Common--Residual Geometry Alignment (CR-GEO) with routed optimization to structure the geometry states while promoting latent-mediated answer learning. CR-GEO separates shared from residual teacher geometry; routed optimization jointly trains geometry and language, temporarily directs visual answer learning through the latents, and restores full attention with geometry supervision. In controlled comparisons, CR-GEO raises geometry effective rank from 1.00 to 3.87, while blocking latent readout at the bottleneck lowers direction accuracy from 89.1% to 25.8% on 128 fixed questions. After recovery, the differentiated geometry representation and latent-mediated visual route remain available alongside direct image access. GeoLatent achieves 73.0% on SPAR-Bench and 72.1% on SPBench, outperforming previously reported methods on both.

</details>


### [158] [Surface-volume self-supervised representation learning of brain MRI for genetic discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02114)
*Tian Xia,Nuo Chen,Zihao Zhu,Huiwen Han,Ziqian Xie,Zhiwen Fan,Degui Zhi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MEVA是一个自监督框架，将体素级图像强度与皮层网格几何（曲率、皮层厚度）编码为共享的影像特征，其GWAS发现的全基因组显著位点多于单独使用体积或网格的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有脑影像GWAS依赖预定义或深度学习衍生的影像表型，但这些表型要么来自体积扫描，要么来自皮层表面网格，各自只能捕捉脑解剖遗传变异的一部分，信息不完整。

Method: 提出MEVA（Mesh-Enhanced Volumetric Autoencoder）自监督框架，将体素级图像强度与皮层网格几何（每个表面顶点的曲率和皮层厚度）联合编码为同一套共享影像特征。

Result: 结合网格与体积输入在年龄和性别预测上相较单一输入模型有小幅提升；在英国生物样本库GWAS中，这些特征比单独来自体积或网格的特征揭示出更多全基因组显著位点。

Conclusion: 在体积自监督学习中引入皮层表面几何可捕捉额外的可遗传变异，从而增加检测到的位点数量。

Abstract: Existing genome-wide association studies (GWAS) of brain imaging provide predefined or deep-learning-derived imaging phenotypes, yet these phenotypes come from either volumetric scans or cortical surface meshes, so each captures only part of the heritable variation in brain anatomy. Here we introduce MEVA (Mesh-Enhanced Volumetric Autoencoder), a self-supervised framework that encodes voxel-level image intensity together with cortical mesh geometry, including curvature and cortical thickness at each surface vertex, into one shared set of imaging features. Combining the mesh and volumetric inputs in MEVA yields modest performance gains in age and sex prediction over models that use either input alone. When these features serve as phenotypes for GWAS in the UK Biobank, they reveal more genome-wide significant loci than features learned from volumes alone or from meshes alone. These results suggest that adding cortical surface geometry to volumetric self-supervised learning captures additional heritable variation and so increases the number of loci detected.

</details>


### [159] [Harnessing Domain Specialists in Multimodal Mixture-of-Experts for Efficient Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02123)
*Damiano Marsili,Raphi Kang,Aditya Mehta,Pietro Perona,Georgia Gkioxari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文发现多模态MoE中的专家会自发形成跨模态与领域的语义专业化，并提出无需数据的ExpertLens方法从预训练权重中识别领域专家，用于高效微调，在多项任务上以更少参数达到或超越全量微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE通过稀疏计算扩展模型容量，但作者想探究这种稀疏性是否会在多模态MoE中产生涌现式的内在组织结构（专家语义专业化），并希望将这种结构用于高效的多模态适配。

Method: 1) 分析预训练多模态MoE专家的语义专业化现象；2) 提出ExpertLens，一种无需数据的方法，通过将路由器权重解码为语义上有意义的词汇token，直接从预训练模型权重中识别领域专家；3) 基于该专业化结构，选择性微调与目标领域相关的专家，实现高效多模态适配。

Result: 在数学、医学和遥感任务上，ExpertLens仅更新21.7%-47.0%的模型参数即可匹配或超越全量微调，获得平均4.0倍训练加速，并在适配性能和训练效率上均优于LoRA。

Conclusion: 为效率而引入的稀疏性可以产生语义模块化，这种模块化可直接用于高效适配，说明MoE的稀疏结构不仅提升效率，还蕴含可解释、可利用的语义组织。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures scale model capacity through sparse computation, routing each token through only a small subset of experts. In this work, we explore whether this sparsity gives rise to emergent intrinsic organization in multimodal MoEs. We find that experts develop strong semantic specialization across modalities and domains despite not being explicitly trained for modularity. Building on this structure, we introduce ExpertLens, a data-free method that identifies domain-specialized experts directly from pretrained model weights by decoding router weights into semantically meaningful vocabulary tokens. We leverage this specialization for efficient multimodal adaptation by selectively fine-tuning experts relevant to a target domain. Across math, medical, and remote sensing tasks, ExpertLens matches or surpasses full fine-tuning while updating only 21.7 - 47.0% of model parameters and achieving a 4.0x average training speedup, and outperforms LoRA in both adaptation performance and training efficiency. These results show that sparsity introduced for efficiency can give rise to semantic modularity that is directly useful for efficient adaptation.

</details>


### [160] [MIRTO: a registration-gated, multiverse-tested evaluation protocol for unsupervised anomaly segmentation in brain MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02136)
*Negin Kafee Hernashki,Soumick Chatterjee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MIRTO 是一个面向脑部 MRI 无监督异常检测（UAD）的评估协议，通过注册检查、验证集阈值、真实假阳性体积、15,552 条评估流水线及带多重性控制的配对 bootstrap，显式量化和报告通常被忽略的评估选择对方法排名的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 UAD 方法仅用单一分数排名，但该分数依赖于很少被报告的评估选择：异常图与参考的对齐方式、阈值设定方式与数据、假阳性预算、度量、聚合方式及病灶定义。这些未报告的选择可能导致结论不可靠甚至错误（如轴序错配使体素 AUROC 从 0.873 降至 0.583 而切片级 AUROC 几乎不变），因此需要一个使评估选择显式化并量化其效应的协议。

Method: MIRTO 使用注册检查约束每次比较的几何一致性，采用无标签诊断检验已知功效；仅在验证数据上设定阈值，并报告测试集上实际实现的假阳性体积；在 15,552 条可辩护的评估流水线上重复每次比较；并给出带多重性控制的配对受试者 bootstrap 区间。对四种在相同健康数据上训练、在 312 名 BraTS 2020 受试者上测试的 UAD 方法进行了应用与验证。

Result: 轴序错配使扩散模型的体素 AUROC 从 0.873 降至 0.583，而切片级 AUROC 几乎不变。在各度量内，方法解释了体素 AUROC 与 AUPRC 至少 0.95 的方差、Dice 0.77 的方差，但对病灶敏感度仅 0.14，后者由病灶定义与命中准则主导。验证阈值下显著的 Dice 优势在等实际假阳性负担下消失，精确恒等式归因于阈值迁移。对 REFLECT 潜变量聚合的无训练改动在等负担下使 Dice 提升 0.052。共检验 9 个假设。

Conclusion: MIRTO 表明 UAD 方法的评估结论高度依赖未报告的评估选择，单分数排名可能具有误导性。由于同一队列既用于开发协议又用于验证，所有推断均为探索性；结果提示需公开报告评估流水线细节并在等假阳性负担下比较方法。

Abstract: Unsupervised anomaly detection (UAD) methods for brain MRI are ranked by a single score, yet that score rests on choices that are rarely reported: how each anomaly map is aligned with the reference, how and on which data the threshold is set, and which false-positive budget, metric, aggregation and lesion definition are used. We present MIRTO, an evaluation protocol that makes these choices explicit and measures their effect. It gates the geometry of every comparison with a registration check and label-free diagnostics of known power, sets thresholds on validation data alone and reports the false-positive volume actually realised on test, repeats each comparison over 15,552 defensible evaluation pipelines, and attaches paired subject-bootstrap intervals with multiplicity control. Applied to four UAD methods trained on the same healthy data and tested on 312 BraTS 2020 subjects, MIRTO showed that an axis-order mismatch between stored maps and the reference lowered a diffusion model's voxel AUROC from 0.873 to 0.583 whilst barely moving its slice-level AUROC. Within each metric, the method explained at least 0.95 of the variance in voxel AUROC and AUPRC and 0.77 in Dice, but only 0.14 in lesion sensitivity, where the lesion definition and hit criterion dominated. A Dice advantage that was significant at validation thresholds vanished at equal realised false-positive burden, and an exact identity attributes it to threshold transfer. A training-free change to REFLECT's latent aggregation raised Dice at equal burden by 0.052. Nine hypotheses were tested against explicit criteria; because the same cohort served to develop the protocol, all inference is exploratory.

</details>


### [161] [Omni-Embed-Mini: Binding Modalities Without Forgetting via Dense Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02148)
*Mohammed Irfan Kurpath,Jaseel Muhammad Kaithakkodan,Sahal Shaji Mullappilly,Ivan Laptev,Hisham Cholakkal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Omni-Embed-Mini 是一种 0.9B 参数的全模态嵌入模型，通过冻结文本侧参数并利用级联字幕作为教师信号，将文本、语音、音频、图像、视频和富视觉文档映射到统一余弦空间，避免文本检索性能退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将文本嵌入模型扩展到新模态通常会损害文本检索质量，而现有全模态嵌入器需要数十亿参数来补偿这一退化。

Method: 每个媒体样本配以稠密级联字幕，教师目标即冻结骨干网络对该字幕的嵌入；因师生共享骨干权重，几何完全一致，仅需轻量投影器和分阶段 LoRA 适配器对齐模态编码器；训练结合 Matryoshka SigLIP 对比损失与在线混合难负样本挖掘器。2.3B 版本使用原生视觉语言骨干。

Result: 0.9B 版本文本权重与骨干逐位一致，MTEB-v2 BEIR-8 达 49.57 nDCG@10，扩展至五种额外模态，比所有对比的开放全模态嵌入器小约 2.7 至 9.5 倍；2.3B 版本与闭源 gemini-embedding-2 相当，在全模态平均上略优。

Conclusion: 该工作证明无需更新文本侧参数即可高效实现全模态对齐，显著降低模型规模并保持文本检索质量，同时在大规模版本上具备与闭源模型竞争的能力。

Abstract: Extending a text embedding model to new modalities typically degrades text retrieval quality, and existing omni-modal embedders compensate with multi-billion parameters. We present Omni-Embed-Mini, a 0.9B-parameter model that maps text, speech, audio, images, video, and visually-rich documents into a single shared cosine space without updating any text-side parameter. Our key insight is that the teacher signal requires no separate embedding model: each media sample is paired with a dense cascaded caption, and the teacher target is simply the frozen backbone's own embedding of that caption. Because teacher and student share the same backbone weights, they inhabit byte-identical geometry, and lightweight projectors plus phased LoRA adapters on the modality encoders suffice for alignment. Training combines a Matryoshka SigLIP contrastive loss with an online hybrid hard-negative miner whose negatives sharpen as the encoder improves. The recipe carries over to a 2.3B variant by swapping in a native vision-language backbone. Omni-Embed-Mini-0.9B keeps its text weights bit-identical to the backbone, so training cannot regress text retrieval (49.57 nDCG@10 on MTEB-v2 BEIR-8), while extending it to five additional modalities, and is ~2.7x to 9.5x smaller than every open omni embedder we compare against. The 2.3B variant is competitive with the closed gemini-embedding-2, edging ahead of it on the overall-modality average. Models, code, data and evaluation harness are on our project page: https://omniembed.cvmbzuai.com

</details>


### [162] [MosaiChunk: Compositing Spatio-Temporal Memory for Autoregressive Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02153)
*Yiwen Zhang,Haocheng Xi,Michael Tian-Yue Liu,Alexei A. Efros,Hadar Averbuch-Elor,Qianqian Wang,Haiwen Feng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 MosaiChunk 时空记忆机制，通过拼贴历史 KV 条目解决长时程自回归视频生成中细节丢失问题，并构建 RememBench 基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程自回归视频生成受限于有限上下文窗口，当物体或场景离开上下文后，其细粒度视觉细节会丢失且难以在重现时恢复。

Method: 基于冻结的视频生成器可直接消费非连续历史 KV 的观察，保持生成器固定，仅学习一个轻量级路由器，在固定活跃记忆预算下决定将哪些历史片段纳入跨时空拼贴。

Result: 在 T2V 与 I2V 两种设定下，MosaiChunk 在相同记忆预算下相比滑动窗口推理和整块检索均持续提升重现一致性；并引入 RememBench 基准（含提示驱动 T2V 与相机驱动 I2V 两个子集）。

Conclusion: 跨时空拼贴历史 KV 并结合轻量路由，可有效保留细粒度视觉细节，提升长时程视频生成的重现一致性。

Abstract: Long-horizon autoregressive video generation is limited by a finite context window. When an object or scene falls out of context, its fine-grained visual details may be lost and difficult to recover upon reappearance. To retain access to such visual details, we introduce MosaiChunk, a spatio-temporal memory mechanism that composes a mosaic of selected historical key-value (KV) entries across space and time. Our approach is motivated by the observation that a frozen video generator can directly consume such non-contiguous historical KV and recover the corresponding visual content. We therefore keep the generator fixed and learn only a lightweight router that determines which historical sections to include in the mosaic under a fixed active-memory budget. We further introduce RememBench, a benchmark of long-horizon revisits with prompt-driven text-to-video (T2V) and camera-driven image-to-video (I2V) splits. Our experiments show that MosaiChunk consistently improves revisit consistency over both sliding-window inference and whole-chunk retrieval under matched memory budgets, across both T2V and I2V settings.

</details>


### [163] [4Director: Controlling Video World Models with Rigid 3D Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02160)
*Wei Cao,Hao Zhang,Vikram Voleti,Yuqun Wu,Mallikarjun B R,Shimon Vainer,Mark Boss,Yaoyao Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 4Director是一个以显式4D场景表示为条件的视频世界模型，通过将物体重构为规范网格并施加刚性变换来实现精确的相机与物体运动控制，并配套构建了RealCOD-Rigid数据集与IG-IoU评估指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专业视频制作需要精确的相机与物体运动控制，但现有方法仅能粗略控制物体：基于图像平面的线索在深度和旋转上存在歧义，基于3D轨迹和blob的方法缺乏完整几何信息且跨视角变化时失去一致性。

Method: 提出4Director，以显式4D场景表示为条件：每个物体从输入图像一次性重构为规范网格，并由每帧一个预设的刚性变换驱动。将受控场景渲染为深度视频，并引入Motion Adapter把几何脚手架转化为视频，同时合成视角一致的外观、光照与非刚性动态。训练方面构建了RealCOD-Rigid数据集（20,774个片段，由自动流程标注刚性3D场景），并引入Identity-Gated IoU（IG-IoU）指标。

Result: 实验表明4Director在视觉质量以及相机和物体控制方面均持续优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 以规范网格与逐帧刚性变换构成的4D表示为条件，可提供直观的3D控制界面并避免未观测几何在每帧被独立重生成；结合深度视频脚手架与Motion Adapter，能够实现视角一致且可控的视频生成。

Abstract: Precise control over camera and object motion is essential for professional video production. Existing methods control objects only coarsely, through image-plane cues that are ambiguous in depth and rotation or through 3D tracks and blobs that lack complete geometry and lose consistency across viewpoint changes. We introduce 4Director, a video world model conditioned on an explicit 4D scene representation: each object is reconstructed once from the input image as a canonical mesh and moved by one prescribed rigid transformation per frame. This representation provides an intuitive 3D control interface and prevents unobserved geometry from being regenerated independently in every frame. We render the controlled scene as a depth video and introduce a Motion Adapter that transforms this geometric scaffold into video while synthesizing view-consistent appearance, illumination, and non-rigid dynamics. For training, we construct RealCOD-Rigid, a new dataset of 20,774 clips annotated with rigid 3D scenes by our automatic pipeline. We further introduce Identity-Gated IoU (IG-IoU), which jointly evaluates adherence to prescribed object motion and preservation of object identity. Experiments demonstrate that 4Director consistently outperforms prior methods in visual quality and in camera and object control.

</details>


### [164] [World Observer: Joint Actor-Observer Generation for Persistent World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02162)
*Hyunwook Choi,Dahyun Chung,Hyunsung Kim,Siyoon Jin,Jinhyeok Choi,Junyoung Seo,Seungryong Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 World Observer，通过解耦“观察”与“行动”，联合生成以智能体为中心的 actor 视角与一个或多个全景 observer，使离开视野的物体仍能持续演化，并在重新进入视野时保持状态一致，同时引入世界空间指标与基准进行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频世界模型是 actor-centric（以行动者为中心）的：一旦物体离开智能体视野，模型就失去其演化的直接证据，往往无法保留其状态与动力学，导致物体重新进入视野时状态不一致。因此需要一种能持续观察视野外区域的世界模型。

Method: 1) 将观察与行动解耦，联合生成一个 perspective actor（智能体中心视角）与一个或多个 panoramic observer（观察选定世界区域的全景观察者）；2) 通过从共享全景源进行 warping，为 actor 和 observers 建立显式几何对应关系；3) 引入 Observer Sink，用高分辨率 perspective 参考在物体重入视野时恢复精细外观；4) observers 可自由放置在场景中、扩展到多个位置以扩大覆盖范围，并可由控制信号驱动以引导视野外演化；5) 提出世界空间指标与跨真实/合成场景的基准。

Result: World Observer 显著提升了视野外动力学（out-of-view dynamics）的建模能力，同时在视觉保真度、相机控制和 3D 一致性方面保持了竞争力。

Conclusion: 通过解耦观察与行动并引入全景观察者机制，World Observer 有效解决了视频世界模型中视野外物体状态丢失的问题，为持续观察与建模整个世界演化提供了可行方案。

Abstract: How can a world model continuously observe regions beyond the actor's current view? Video world models simulate how an environment evolves from an agent's actions, yet remain actor-centric. Once an object leaves the actor's view, they lose direct evidence of its evolution, often failing to preserve its state and dynamics upon re-entry. To address this, we introduce World Observer, which decouples observing from acting by jointly generating a perspective actor for the agent-centric view with one or more panoramic observers that watch selected world regions. This allows objects that leave the actor's view to remain visually evolving in an observer, so their updated states are reflected when they re-enter. We ground the actor and observers by warping from a shared panoramic source for explicit geometric correspondence, and introduce an Observer Sink of high-resolution perspective references to restore fine appearance upon re-entry. Since the observers are decoupled from the actor, they can be placed freely across the scene, extended to multiple locations for broader coverage, and driven by control signals to steer out-of-view evolution. To evaluate out-of-view evolution, we further introduce world-space metrics and a benchmark spanning real and synthetic scenes. World Observer substantially improves out-of-view dynamics while remaining competitive in visual fidelity, camera control, and 3D adherence.

</details>


### [165] [Generative Cinematographer: Composing Camera and Object Motion in 3D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02180)
*Jiahan Zhang,Chaohao Yang,Namitha Guruprasad,Vivekjyoti Banerjee,Trong-Tung Nguyen,Alan Yuille,Anand Bhattad*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GenCine 将单张图像提升为可编辑的3D场景支架，让艺术家在同一世界坐标系中联合编排相机与前景运动，并通过引导图将控制信号传递给预训练视频模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可控视频生成依赖2D运动轨迹或稀疏拖拽信号，存在歧义：同一2D轨迹可能对应不同3D运动，尤其在相机与物体同时运动时难以区分。

Method: 将单图提升为可编辑3D场景支架，用局部3D运动手柄移动前景区域（多手柄可独立控制，近似分段刚性非刚体运动，无需物理模拟器或类别先验）；将相机路径与手柄运动投影为引导图（记录受控区域每帧位置，为各手柄分配固定颜色，并在与背景相同的世界坐标系中编码受控点的3D位置）；训练时从真实视频恢复控制信号、用合成视频提供真值几何与轨迹，在预训练Wan模型上训练轻量引导分支与LoRA适配器。

Result: 实验显示在多种真实场景中实现一致的相机相对运动、视角变化下更好的几何一致性，以及较强的可控性。

Conclusion: 通过3D场景支架与引导图将相机与前景运动解耦并联合控制，GenCine 在预训练视频模型上实现了更一致、几何更可靠的相机相对运动控制。

Abstract: Current controllable video generation systems often rely on 2D motion trajectories or sparse drag signals for object motion. These controls are ambiguous because the same 2D trajectory can correspond to different 3D motions, especially when the camera and objects move simultaneously. We present Generative Cinematographer (GenCine), a system that lifts a single image into an editable 3D scene scaffold where artists jointly author camera and foreground motion. Artists specify a camera path and move selected foreground regions using local 3D motion handles. Several handles can move different parts of a subject independently, providing a piecewise-rigid approximation to non-rigid motion without a physics simulator or category-specific prior. To communicate these controls to a pretrained video model, we project them into guidance maps. These maps record where the controlled regions appear in each frame, assign each handle a fixed color across frames and encode the current 3D positions of its controlled points in the same world coordinate system as the background. This lets us describe object motion relative to the scene even as the camera moves. For training, we recover controls from the motion observed in real videos and use ground-truth geometry and trajectories from synthetic videos. We train a lightweight guidance branch and LoRA adapters on a pretrained Wan model to follow these controls. Our experiments show consistent camera-relative motion, improved geometric consistency under viewpoint changes, and strong controllability across diverse real-world scenes.

</details>


### [166] [OmniSeek: Native Tool Integration for Multi-turn Audio-Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02181)
*Haibo Wang,Jiteng Mu,Jialu Li,Jingru Yi,Yuanjun Xiong,Jianming Zhang,Lifu Huang,Mingze Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OmniSeek 是一个智能体框架，将全模态大模型转化为具备原生工具调用能力的主动式多轮推理智能体，通过动态检索音视频证据提升长上下文下的跨模态推理表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统全模态大模型是被动地在单次前向传播中处理整段音视频序列，难以在长上下文中定位稀疏但关键的多模态证据，导致推理效率与准确性受限。

Method: 提出 OmniSeek 框架，让证据获取成为推理过程的一部分：模型动态决定"看"还是"听"以及时间窗口，通过迭代多轮协议将检索到的原始音视频片段追加回上下文。冷启动方面，构建数据引擎合成 OmniTraj-170K（含交错音视频证据的多跳思维链轨迹），先监督学习注入多轮工具使用行为，再用可验证奖励的两阶段强化学习优化策略，并引入音视频必要性目标鼓励双模态依赖、抑制单模态捷径。

Result: 在广泛的基准测试中，OmniSeek 学会了自适应的跨模态证据检索，并持续提升音视频推理性能。

Conclusion: 将主动式证据获取融入推理过程，是提升全模态大模型长上下文跨模态推理能力的有效途径。

Abstract: We present OmniSeek, an agentic framework that transforms an Omni Large Language Model (Omni-LLM) into an active, multi-turn reasoning agent with native tool use. Rather than passively processing an entire audio-visual sequence in a single forward pass, OmniSeek makes evidence acquisition part of the reasoning process: it dynamically decides whether to look or listen, and over which temporal window, to retrieve sparse but critical evidence across different modalities within long contexts. Through an iterative multi-turn protocol, the retrieved raw audio or visual segments are appended back into the context to support subsequent reasoning. To cold-start this capability, we build a data engine that synthesizes OmniTraj-170K, a corpus of multi-hop Chain-of-Thought trajectories with interleaved audio and visual evidence. We first supervise the model on these trajectories to instill multi-turn tool-use behavior, and then further optimize the policy via a two-stage reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards. Moreover, we introduce an Audio-Visual Necessity objective that explicitly rewards successful trajectories whose reasoning depends on both modalities, discouraging single-modality shortcuts. Extensive experiments across a wide range of benchmarks demonstrate that OmniSeek learns adaptive cross-modal evidence seeking and consistently improves audio-visual reasoning performance.

</details>


### [167] [DMAD: Distribution Matching as Adversarial Distillation for Fast Visual Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02188)
*Zhengming Yu,Junkun Yuan,Haotian Yang,Gordon Guocheng Qian,Yizhi Wang,Angtian Wang,Yiding Yang,Bo Liu,Xin Li,Wenping Wang,Chongyang Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DMAD 将分布匹配蒸馏重新表述为分类任务，通过在共享骨干上设置两个判别器头直接学习对数密度比，避免了 DMD 中维持辅助扩散模型的额外开销，并证明了其在判别器最优时恢复 DMD 的分布匹配梯度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DMD 需要保持一个辅助扩散模型来拟合学生不断演化的分布，带来额外的内存与计算成本。为降低这一开销，需要一种无需辅助分数拟合即可实现分布匹配的方法。

Method: 提出 DMAD，将分布匹配重铸为分类问题。在共享骨干上使用两个判别器头，区分真实数据与教师样本和学生样本，通过在判别器 logits 上应用线性损失来训练学生，无需辅助分数拟合。还引入基于 gap 的重加权，利用真实数据头在真实与教师样本间的经验 logit gap 自适应调整不同噪声水平下的教师监督。

Result: 在 ImageNet-64x64 上单步生成 FID 达 1.04；在 COCO-10K 上四步 SDXL 达 14.47；四步 Wan2.1-T2V-14B 的 VBench 总分达 85.15，均为所比较的少步方法及多步教师中的最佳值。在 MiniMax-H3-33B 上，四步学生在联合音视频生成中相对 DMD2 的人类偏好率为 79.1%，相对 rCM 为 84.6%（不计平局）。

Conclusion: DMAD 通过分类式的判别器设计直接学习对数密度比，理论证明其在判别器最优时恢复 DMD 的分布匹配梯度，并在图像与视频生成任务上取得优于少步方法与多步教师的性能。

Abstract: Distribution Matching Distillation (DMD) trains a few-step student from the difference between separately estimated target and student scores, so it must keep an auxiliary diffusion model fitted to the student's evolving distribution at extra memory and computation cost. We introduce DMAD, Distribution Matching as Adversarial Distillation, which recasts distribution matching as classification and learns the required log-density ratios directly. Two discriminator heads on a shared backbone distinguish real data and teacher samples from the student's, and linear losses on their logits train the student without auxiliary score fitting. We prove that at the discriminator optimum these losses recover the distribution-matching gradient underlying DMD, through the classical identity linking discriminator logits to log-density ratios. We further introduce gap-based reweighting, which adapts teacher supervision across noise levels from the real-data head's empirical logit gap between real and teacher samples. DMAD reaches a Fréchet Inception Distance (FID) of 1.04 with one-step generation on ImageNet-64x64, 14.47 with four-step SDXL on COCO-10K, and a VBench total score of 85.15 with four-step Wan2.1-T2V-14B, the best values among the compared few-step methods and the multi-step teachers. On MiniMax-H3-33B, our four-step student achieves overall human preference rates of 79.1% over DMD2 and 84.6% over rCM for joint audio-video generation, excluding ties. Our code, models and demos are available at https://yzmblog.github.io/projects/DMAD.

</details>


### [168] [HiPhy: Hierarchical Alignment for Physically-Plausible Multi-Principle Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02197)
*Tahira Kazimi,Shubhankar Borse,Munawar Hayat,Fatih Porikli,Pinar Yanardag*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出HiPhy，一个基于强化学习的视频生成框架，通过局部与全局双层目标将物理规律嵌入生成过程，并构建50K提示数据集与MultiPhyBench基准，在多物理原理共现场景中显著提升物理常识与语义对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成模型视觉保真度高、有潜力成为通用世界模拟器，但仍难以生成符合物理规律的视频。尤其真实场景中多种物理原理需同时协同作用（如气球上浮与锅上蒸汽升腾需浮力与流体动力学同时连贯展开），而现有方法多聚焦单一物理原理，忽视了多原理交互。

Method: 提出HiPhy（Hierarchical Physical Alignment，层次化物理对齐）强化学习框架，采用双层目标：局部层面约束单个物理原理的时间动态，全局层面保证整个场景的物理与语义一致性。为支持多原理生成，构建包含50K提示的数据集，并引入多物理事件共现的提示基准MultiPhyBench。

Result: 实验表明HiPhy显著优于先前方法与基线，在多个基准上大幅提升物理常识与语义对齐，且在涉及多个并发物理原理的场景中提升最大——而这正是竞争方法退化最严重的场景。

Conclusion: HiPhy通过层次化物理对齐有效缓解了多物理原理共现时的生成难题，为物理一致的视频生成与世界模拟器研究提供了新思路、数据集与评测基准。

Abstract: Video generation models have achieved remarkable visual fidelity and have strong potential to become general-purpose world simulators. Despite this progress, they still fail to generate videos which adhere to laws of physics. The problem becomes even more apparent in realistic settings where multiple physical principles must work together within the same video; for example, "a balloon floating upward while steam rises from a pot" requires buoyancy and fluid dynamics to unfold coherently and simultaneously. Yet existing methods largely ignore multi-principle interactions, focusing on a single principle per video. We propose HiPhy (Hierarchical Physical Alignment), a reinforcement learning framework that grounds video generation in physical laws through a dual-level objective: locally enforcing the temporal dynamics of individual physical principles, and globally ensuring the physical and semantic coherence of the entire scene. To support multi-principle generation, we construct a 50K-prompt dataset and introduce a prompt benchmark MultiPhyBench, spanning a diverse range of co-occurring physical events. Our experiments show that HiPhy significantly outperforms prior methods and baselines, improving physical commonsense and semantic alignment significantly across various benchmarks, with the largest gains on scenes involving multiple concurrent physical principles where competing methods degrade most sharply.

</details>


### [169] [SILSA: Sliding-Window Slice Latents for Topology-Preserving High-Resolution 3D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02201)
*Tianjiao Yu,Xinzhuo Li,Yifan Shen,Ying Shen,Kiet A. Nguyen,Adheesh Sunil Juvekar,Ismini Lourentzou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SILSA 是一个拓扑感知的 3D 生成框架，用紧凑的滑动窗口切片隐变量替代昂贵的体素 token，实现单阶段生成并显著降低成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率 3D 生成依赖体素隐变量和多阶段流水线（先预测结构再合成局部几何），将连续表面碎片化为大量局部 token，导致生成成本膨胀，且对薄结构或高连通形状的拓扑一致性较差。

Method: 提出 SILSA：用固定的一组沿三个标准轴重叠的切片表示形状，每个 token 概括局部深度窗口以保持截面连续性并支持单阶段 rectified-flow 生成；Slice VAE 将定向表面采样编码为多轴切片隐变量，并用稀疏体解码器重建；Volumetric Anchor Lattice 通过共享 3D 工作空间协调各方向切片流；引入切片级拓扑监督，匹配持久图并对齐相邻切片的 Betti 变化。

Result: 相比最强基线，PSNR 提升 8.7%，覆盖率提升 5.96 个绝对点，Betti 误差降低 9.2%；相比次紧凑基线减少 70.0% token，相比稀疏或层级 tokenizer 减少超 98% token，训练内存降低 40.4%，推理时间减少 58.5%；定性结果在薄结构、重复部件和长程连通性上表现更好。

Conclusion: SILSA 通过切片隐变量与切片级拓扑监督，在提升结构保真度的同时大幅降低生成成本，兼顾效率与拓扑正确性。

Abstract: High-resolution 3D generation increasingly relies on voxel latents and multi-stage pipelines that first predict active structure and then synthesize local geometry. While effective, this design fragments continuous surfaces into many local tokens, inflates generation cost, and often weakens topological consistency for thin or highly connected shapes. We introduce SILSA, a topology-aware 3D generation framework that represents shapes with compact sliding-window slice latents. Instead of generating expensive voxel tokens, SILSA uses a fixed set of overlapping slices along the three canonical axes, where each token summarizes a local depth window to preserve cross-sectional continuity and support single-stage rectified-flow generation. A Slice VAE encodes oriented surface samples into multi-axis slice latents and reconstructs them with a sparse volumetric decoder, while a Volumetric Anchor Lattice coordinates directional slice streams through a shared 3D workspace. To preserve structural correctness, we introduce slice-level topology supervision that matches persistence diagrams and aligns Betti transitions across neighboring slices. Experiments show that SILSA improves structural fidelity while substantially reducing generation cost. SILSA improves PSNR by $8.7\%$, coverage by $5.96$ absolute points, and Betti error by $9.2\%$ over the strongest baseline, while using $70.0\%$ fewer tokens than the next-most compact baseline and over $98\%$ fewer tokens than sparse or hierarchical tokenizers, effectively reducing training memory by $40.4\%$ and inference time by $58.5\%$. Qualitative results further show improved preservation of thin structures, repeated components, and long-range connectivity.

</details>


### [170] [Embedding Prediction Helps Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02203)
*Sihan Xu,Ji Xie,Zilin Wang,Hui Shen,Stella X. Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出NEPA，用预测的连续嵌入替代固定的类别标签/文本提示作为扩散Transformer的条件，在每个去噪步重新计算，NEPA-DiT-XL在ImageNet 256×256上达到FID 1.32，训练算力约为REPA的三分之一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统扩散Transformer中，类别标签或文本提示只被嵌入一次，并在每个去噪步重复使用同一条件，条件信号无法随当前含噪状态自适应变化。作者希望探索是否可以用预测出的嵌入作为条件，使条件在去噪过程中动态更新。

Method: 提出NEPA（Next-Embedding Predictive Autoregression）：训练Transformer预测序列中的下一个连续嵌入。生成时干净图像位于含噪图像之后，其嵌入即为条件与含噪图像之后的下一个嵌入。通过Multi-Embedding Prediction一次性预测所有嵌入；在Embedding Conditioned Generation中，DiT生成器以这些预测为条件，并在每个去噪步重新计算，使条件信号适应当前含噪状态。在类别条件ImageNet 256×256上研究生成器条件、Multi-Embedding Prediction设计以及两个模型的扩展性。

Result: NEPA为每个采样步增加第二个网络；结合该网络与REPA，最终模型NEPA-DiT-XL达到FID 1.32，训练算力约为REPA的三分之一。

Conclusion: 预测嵌入作为扩散Transformer的动态条件可行且有效，能在显著降低训练算力的同时提升生成质量（FID），为扩散生成模型的条件机制提供了新的思路。

Abstract: In diffusion transformers, a class label or a text prompt is embedded once, and the same condition is reused at every denoising step. We ask whether predicted embeddings can serve as this condition instead. Next-Embedding Predictive Autoregression (NEPA) trains a Transformer to predict the next continuous embedding in a sequence. In generation, the clean image follows the noisy image, so its embeddings are the next embeddings after the condition and the noisy image. We train a NEPA model to predict them all at once with Multi-Embedding Prediction, and in Embedding Conditioned Generation, a DiT generator is conditioned on these predictions, recomputed at every denoising step, so the conditioning signal adapts to the current noisy state. Experiments on class-conditional ImageNet $256\times256$ study the condition of the generator, the design of Multi-Embedding Prediction, and the scaling of both models. The NEPA model adds a second network to every sampling step; with it, and combined with REPA, our final model, NEPA-DiT-XL, reaches an FID of 1.32 using about a third of the training compute of REPA.

</details>


### [171] [ROWBench: Do Video Models Render What the Program Specifies?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02205)
*Zheng-Hui Huang,Guixu Lin,Yu-Ju Tsai,Jian-Kai Zhu,Fengbo Lan,Yu-Lun Liu,Yung-Yu Chuang,Kaipeng Zhang,Zhixiang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PROWBench，一个用于评估程序化世界模型视觉保真度的基准，包含170个程序化构建的片段与600个代理视频，通过记录实体状态和时间戳事件来检验生成视频是否符合程序执行的可观察结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 程序化世界模型将可执行动力学与视觉生成分离，但其对显式规则和交互的视觉遵循程度评估不足；现有基准多评估视觉质量、可控性或物理遵循，很少测试对细粒度、程序指定世界事件的保真度。

Method: 构建PROWBench，含170个程序化片段和600个代理视频，覆盖多样场景与交互；记录实体状态和时间戳事件（包括视野外事件）作为可重放世界记录，并渲染同步视图与代理表示；提供可扩展框架构建场景、控制行为，支持不同表征（如粗略3D、边界框）；覆盖第一和第三人称视角，部分片段提供同步多视角观察；基于记录评估实体控制、长时程记忆，并使用两个VLM指标（Logic-Render Alignment和Interaction Success Rate）评估时间线遵循与事件视觉实现。

Result: PROWBench能够将生成视频与程序执行的可观察结果进行对照检查，覆盖实体控制、长时程记忆以及对规定时间线和引擎记录事件视觉实现的遵循情况。

Conclusion: PROWBench为程序化世界模型提供了细粒度、程序指定事件的视觉保真度评估手段，弥补了现有基准在规则与交互视觉遵循评估方面的不足。

Abstract: Programmable world models separate executable dynamics from visual generation, offering a promising foundation for next-generation game engines. However, their visual adherence to explicit rules and interactions remains insufficiently evaluated. Existing benchmarks assess visual quality, controllability, and instruction or physical adherence, but rarely test fidelity to fine-grained, program-specified world events. We introduce PROWBench, comprising 170 programmatically constructed episodes and 600 proxy videos covering diverse scenes and interactions. PROWBench logs entity states and timestamped events, including those outside the camera's field of view, as replayable world records, from which it renders synchronized views and proxy representations. This enables generated videos to be checked against the observable consequences of program execution. An extensible framework constructs scenes, controls behaviors, and can render each camera view in different representations, such as coarse 3D, and bounding boxes. The benchmark covers first- and third-person perspectives, with synchronized multi-view observations available for a subset of episodes. Grounded in these records, PROWBench evaluates entity control, long-horizon memory, and, with two VLM-based metrics, Logic-Render Alignment and Interaction Success Rate, adherence to the prescribed timeline and the visual realization of timestamped engine-recorded events.

</details>


### [172] [One Basis to Animate Them All: Gaussian Blendshape Distillation for Real-Time Avatars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02207)
*Ramazan Fazylov,Stamatis Lefkimmiatis,Ivan Laptev*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出GALA方法，通过线性近似将预训练3D高斯化身模型的动画解码蒸馏为浅层系数预测器加线性混合，实现移动端高达60fps的实时动画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D高斯化身渲染快，但实时动画受制于昂贵的神经推理开销，需要一种既能保持渲染质量又能大幅降低计算成本的方法。

Method: 基于"预训练化身模型的动画可被身份无关的blendshape线性组合近似"这一发现，GALA用浅层MLP系数预测器和线性混合替代逐帧重神经解码；并采用块局部PCA，在渲染感知度量与内存预算下构建基底，无需重新训练原模型。

Result: 在三个不同化身模型（面部表情与带衣物动态的全身动画）上验证，蒸馏可泛化到未见身份，CPU动画开销最多降低三个数量级，同时保留大部分渲染质量，移动端帧率达60fps。

Conclusion: 结果证实学习到的化身表示具有共享线性结构，可实现高效且准确的实时动画。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian avatars support fast rendering, however, their real-time animation is often challenged by the costly neural inference. We address this bottleneck and show that the animation of pretrained avatar models can be closely approximated by a linear combination of identity-independent blendshapes. Building on this finding, we introduce GALA (Gaussian Animation via Linear Approximation), a distillation method that replaces per-frame heavy neural decoding with a shallow coefficient predictor and a linear blend. To improve fidelity and reduce memory requirements, we propose to construct the basis using block-local PCA under a rendering-aware metric and a memory budget. Our method learns a shallow MLP network to predict blendshape coefficients and applies to various animation architectures without retraining original models. We validate GALA by accelerating the inference of three distinct avatar models for 3D animation of facial expressions and full-bodies with clothing dynamics. Across these models, our distillation generalizes to held-out identities and reduces CPU animation cost by up to three orders of magnitude while preserving most of the rendering quality. Excellent results of our method confirm the shared linear structure of learned avatar representations and enable highly efficient and accurate animation at frame rates reaching up to 60fps on mobile devices. Project page: https://ramazan793.github.io/gala/

</details>


### [173] [Sphere Encoder 2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02208)
*Kaiyu Yue,Sean McLeish,Ruchit Rawal,Brian Bartoldson,Menglin Jia,Tom Goldstein*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Sphere Encoder 是一种自编码器，通过从高维潜在球面解码随机点来生成图像。本文指出其两点局限并提出了 Sphere Encoder 2 加以改进，在保持自编码器速度与简洁性的同时显著提升了生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始 Sphere Encoder 的生成质量受两点局限影响：其一，随机点在编码潜空间中集中于赤道附近而非极点，但训练旋转从未触及该区域，留下空缺，限制了单步生成；其二，使用逐像素重建损失训练生成会导致解码器对多个合理图像取平均，产生缺乏高频细节的模糊图像。

Method: 本文提出 Sphere Encoder 2，针对上述两个局限进行改进：一是解决赤道附近随机点与训练旋转未覆盖区域之间的缺口问题，二是缓解逐像素重建损失导致的模糊问题。

Result: Sphere Encoder 2 在保持自编码器速度与简洁性的前提下，显著提升了图像生成质量。

Conclusion: 通过识别并解决原公式的两点局限，Sphere Encoder 2 实现了生成质量的大幅提升，模型已在指定 GitHub 地址发布。

Abstract: Sphere Encoder is an autoencoder that generates images by decoding random points from a high-dimensional latent sphere. We identify two limitations of the original formulation that reduce its generation quality. First, random points concentrate near the equator relative to the pole on an encoded latent, but the training rotation never reaches this region, leaving a gap that limits one-step generation. Second, training for generation with pixel-wise reconstruction loss encourages the decoder to average over plausible images, producing blurry images that lack high-frequency details. We present Sphere Encoder 2 to address both limitations, substantially improving image generation quality while maintaining the speed and simplicity of a autoencoder. Models are released at \href{https://github.com/kaiyuyue/sphere2}{github.com/kaiyuyue/sphere2}.

</details>


### [174] [Moore, Escher, Penrose: A Conformal Golden Braid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02210)
*Sophia Feldman,Assaf Shocher*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出在文本到图像扩散模型的采样过程中，将非可逆图像变换 T 及其广义逆 T† 与去噪步骤交织，让场景内容与几何扭曲共同演化，从而生成类似埃舍尔《Print Gallery》的自指递归图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 埃舍尔《Print Gallery》(1956) 的奇幻自指结构在近半个世纪后被数学分析刻画为共形幂映射 z ↦ z^α。作者希望借助现代扩散模型自动生成这类自指场景，但发现仅靠提示词无法实现递归，事后变换会造成结构连接不良，采样中直接施加变换也会被去噪器"修复"或偏离既定几何。因此需要一个能在生成过程中保持递归几何约束的方法。

Method: 针对不可逆变换 T，作者构造了适配递归约束的广义逆 T†，利用 Penrose 恒等式 TT†T=T 使 TT† 成为到几何可行图像的幂等投影。由于仅对变换后图像去噪仍属分布外任务，作者将去噪步骤与 T 和 T† 交织（braid）：源空间步骤发展未扭曲的场景，变换空间步骤精修其外观与连接，使场景与扭曲同步发展。

Result: 利用冻结的文本到图像扩散模型成功生成了类似《Print Gallery》的自指构图，并进一步探索了其他变换，验证了在采样中交织 T 与 T† 可有效实现递归几何结构。

Conclusion: 与其对完成图像施加扭曲，不如让场景与其扭曲在生成过程中共同发展。该方法为扩散模型中的几何约束生成提供了新思路，并可推广到更广泛的变换类型。

Abstract: I don't think I have ever done anything as peculiar in my life. Among other things, it shows a young man looking with interest at a print on the wall of an exhibition that features himself. How can this be? Perhaps I am not far removed from Einstein's curved universe.'' So wrote M.C. Escher about his 1956 lithograph Print Gallery. Nearly half a century later, a mathematical analysis related its geometry to an untwisted source image through a conformal power map $z \mapsto z^α$, $α\in \mathbb{C}$. Building on this construction, we use a frozen text-to-image diffusion model to generate new self-referential scenes. Prompting alone does not enforce the recursion, while a post-hoc transformation can leave structures poorly connected. Applying the transformation during sampling is also insufficient: the denoiser may "repair" the intended distortion or drift out of the prescribed geometry. We construct a generalized inverse $T^\dagger$ of the non-invertible image transformation $T$, adapted to its recursive constraint. In the idealized formulation, the Penrose identity $TT^\dagger T = T$ makes $TT^\dagger$ an idempotent projection onto geometrically admissible images. Yet denoising only the transformed image remains an out-of-distribution task, even with projection. We therefore braid denoising steps with $T$ and $T^\dagger$: source-space steps develop the untwisted scene, while transformed-space steps refine its appearance and connections in the final geometry. We generate Print Gallery-like compositions and explore further transformations. Rather than distorting a finished image, we let the scene and its distortion develop together.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [175] [On-Device Named-Entity Recognition: A Deployability Study of Accuracy, Cost, Reliability, and Confidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00007)
*Vinay Kumar Chaganti*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统评测9个命名实体识别（NER）系统在三种范式、13M–8B参数规模下的可部署性：不仅报告准确率，还首次提供延迟与输出有效性两个维度，并用跨家族LLM评委面板构建银标准金标来评估无人工标注场景，同时刻画GLiNER的逐跨度置信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实践者需要端侧（无API、低延迟、数据本地）NER，但真正关心的问题不是排行榜，而是三个联合问题：哪个模型可部署、如何在无人工标注下评估、其置信度是否可信。现有文献只报告准确率，遗漏了延迟和输出有效性这两条关键维度。

Method: 在三个性质不同的数据集上评测九种系统，覆盖三种范式：经典标注器spaCy、双向编码器专家GLiNER（166–460M）、本地运行的生成式LLM（Qwen3-0.6B/1.7B/4B-Instruct、DeepSeek-R1-1.5B/8B），参数跨13M至8B。因自建语料（RSS-News）无金标，用跨家族LLM评委面板构建银标准金标，再以基准金标和全量人工复验衡量其保真度（严格F1 0.95，且为上限值，因人工金标以银标为种子）。此外刻画GLiNER逐跨度置信度（AUROC、ECE、温度缩放、阈值化、级联路由）。

Result: 仅看准确率，4B instruct LLM具竞争力（在干净新闻通稿上领先）；但编码器的优势在可部署性：以1/9–1/24的体积匹配或略逊，毫秒至秒级延迟，且零畸形输出，而最小生成模型在长输入上畸形输出高达27%，该失败由规模而非输出预算决定。金标来源会翻转范式排名：从LLM撰写的银标转至人工金标，所有编码器提升、所有生成模型下降。GLiNER置信度能良好排序正确性（AUROC 0.76–0.86）但过度自信（ECE 0.24–0.47，温度缩放可减半）；阈值化带来小幅诚实的样本外F1增益；全本地小到大级联相较成本匹配随机路由有适度且依赖语料的增益；置信度跟踪正确性但不跟踪新颖性。所有数字可由逐跨度记录离线复算。

Conclusion: 端侧NER的选型不应只看准确率；编码器在可部署性（体积、延迟、输出有效性）上占优，生成模型需依赖规模才能消除畸形输出。无金标时跨家族LLM评委面板可构建高保真银标，但其来源会左右范式排名，故需谨慎。置信度可用于排序与阈值化，但数值偏过度自信，需校准；且置信度无法指示新颖性。

Abstract: Named-entity recognition (NER) is increasingly wanted on-device (no API, low latency, data kept local). The practitioner's question is not the leaderboard but which model is deployable, how to evaluate it without human annotation, and whether its confidence can be trusted. We answer these jointly. We place nine systems across three paradigms and 13 M to 8 B parameters: a classical tagger (spaCy), bidirectional-encoder specialists (GLiNER, 166 to 460 M), and generative LLMs run locally (Qwen3-0.6B/1.7B/4B-Instruct, DeepSeek-R1-1.5B/8B), on three datasets of differing character, and report accuracy plus two axes the literature omits: latency and output validity. Because our corpus (RSS-News) had no gold, we built silver gold from a cross-family LLM judge panel, then measured its fidelity against benchmark gold and a full human re-validation of the corpus (strict F1 0.95, an upper bound since the human gold was silver-seeded); gold provenance flips the paradigm ranking, moving from LLM-authored silver to human gold raises every encoder and lowers every generative model.
