Across 61 simulation worlds, SciUtopia simulates over 40,000 researchers from 8,000 institutions, producing around 400,000 publication decisions and 1.2 million LLM-generated peer reviews. Using these longitudinal simulations, we find that rejection-driven resubmission substantially amplifies reviewer burden beyond population growth alone, cautious exploration balances citation impact with career success and long-term topic diversity, and resource inequality can emerge even without detectable cumulative advantage from narrowly winning early funding. Code is available at https://github.com/Ahren09/ScienceUtopia.

</details>


### [247] [Know When to Hold 'em: Correct-Token Retention in Uniform-State Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01275)
*Mojtaba Nafez,James Henderson*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 当前统一状态扩散模型（USDM）虽能自我纠错，却缺乏保留正确 token 的能力；作者提出 CTR-Reg 辅助损失解决该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一状态扩散模型（USDMs）可在任意去噪步修正任意 token，具备自我纠错优势，但自我纠错既需修正错误 token，也需保留正确 token，而现有 USDMs 缺失后者。即使使用 greedy-tail 解码，DUO、UDLM、uniform-noise SEDD 等 SOTA 模型每步仍修改 512 个位置中的 173–270 个，这种大规模无协调的编辑会破坏样本多样性。

Method: 通过随机 token 损坏实验定位问题根源：模型对干净 token 与损坏 token 的重建精度几乎相同，而干净 token 本应更易重建。对验证 NELBO 的分解显示，训练几乎不奖励"保留"行为——错误预测在损坏位置受到重罚，但在干净位置几乎无代价。据此提出 Correct-Token Retention Regularization（CTR-Reg），一种简单有效的辅助损失，训练模型保留前向过程未被扰动的 token，且无需修改采样器。

Result: CTR-Reg 在六个基准上平均提升干净 token 准确率 26.5 个百分点，同时损坏 token 准确率基本不变，每步修改量收敛至仅 3–11 个位置。在仅 5 步 greedy-tail 采样下，三种模型的生成困惑度均下降超过一半，且多样性得以保留，这些收益在不同采样预算下均成立。

Conclusion: 研究识别出"正确 token 保留"是自纠错扩散语言模型缺失的关键要素，并证明 CTR-Reg 是一种有效的修复方法。

Abstract: Uniform-state diffusion models (USDMs) can revise any token at any denoising step, which lets them correct their own mistakes, a key advantage over masked diffusion. Self-correction, however, requires both revising incorrect tokens and retaining correct ones, and we show that current USDMs lack the latter. Even under greedy-tail decoding, state-of-the-art USDMs (DUO, UDLM, and uniform-noise SEDD) keep revising 173--270 of 512 positions at every step, and these large, uncoordinated edits collapse sample diversity. A random-token corruption experiment traces this deficit to the models themselves: they reconstruct clean and corrupted tokens with nearly identical accuracy, even though clean tokens are easier targets. A decomposition of the validation NELBO shows that training barely rewards retention: incorrect predictions are heavily penalized at corrupted positions but almost free at clean ones. We propose Correct-Token Retention Regularization (CTR-Reg), a simple but effective auxiliary loss that trains the model to retain tokens left unperturbed by the forward process and requires no change to the sampler. CTR-Reg improves clean-token accuracy by 26.5 percentage points on average across six benchmarks, while leaving corrupted-token accuracy virtually unchanged, and its per-step revisions converge to only 3--11 positions. With just five greedy-tail steps, generative perplexity more than halves under CTR-Reg for all three models while diversity is preserved, and these gains hold across sampling budgets. Our results identify correct-token retention as a key missing ingredient for self-correcting diffusion language models, and demonstrate an effective fix.

</details>


### [248] [What Wins a Vote? Formatting, Length, and Lexical Diversity in the French Compar:IA LLM Arena](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01316)
*Simonas Zilinskas,Maayeesha Farzana,Christophe Benavent*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过对法语LLM竞技场13.7万次投票进行文体计量分析，发现格式与词汇多样性等呈现特征与胜率相关，调整后排名变动显著，但调整后排名并不更好地反映能力，建议同时公布原始与调整后排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM竞技场将人类成对偏好转化为模型排名，但偏好可能反映答案的呈现方式而非内容本身。作者希望量化格式、长度、可读性、词汇多样性与句法结构等呈现因素对胜率与排名的影响，并检验调整后排名是否更准确地衡量模型能力。

Method: 采用文体计量学方法，分析2026年7月Compar:IA发布的137,293条决定性法语投票；主格式分析涵盖116个模型的137,113场对战，联合估计使用具备全部测量值的127,092场对战。对每场对战重建用户投票时可见的回复，并将原始排名与分别调整格式、长度、可读性、词汇多样性和句子结构后的排名进行比较。

Result: 呈现特征与获胜相关，但长度、加粗文本和列表常同时出现，难以分离各自贡献。在联合模型中，跨设定变化最小的两个关联是：加粗使用（每标准差胜算+11.0%）与移动平均类符形符比MATTR（词汇多样性指标，对长度不敏感，+16.8%）。加粗关联在真实多轮对话中显著减小，而MATTR关联变化不大；由于用户自主选择是否继续对话，该差异仅为描述性而非因果性。完整调整使116个模型中有36个排名变动至少10位。然而与外部基准的比较并未显示调整后排名更好地衡量能力。

Conclusion: 鉴于调整排名并未被证明更能反映模型能力，建议将原始排名与调整后排名并排发布，作为透明的敏感性分析，而非用调整排名替代原始排名。

Abstract: LLM arenas turn pairwise human preferences into model rankings. Those preferences may reflect how an answer is presented as well as what it says. We take a stylometric approach to 137,293 decisive French-language votes from the July 2026 Compar:IA release; the primary formatting analysis includes 137,113 battles across 116 models, and the joint estimates use the 127,092 battles with all required measurements. For each battle, we reconstruct the response visible when the user voted. We then compare the raw ranking with rankings adjusted for formatting, length, readability, vocabulary variety, and sentence structure. Presentation is associated with winning, but length, bold text, and lists tend to occur together, making their individual contributions hard to separate. Across the measured features, two associations change least across specifications: bold usage (+11.0% win odds per standard deviation in the joint model) and moving-average type-token ratio (MATTR), a measure of vocabulary variety that is less sensitive to answer length (+16.8%). The bold association is substantially smaller in observed multi-turn conversations, whereas the MATTR association changes little; because users choose whether to continue, this difference is descriptive rather than causal. The full adjustment moves 36 of 116 models by at least ten ranks. Yet comparisons with external benchmarks do not show that adjusted rankings better measure capability. We therefore recommend publishing raw and adjusted rankings side by side as a transparent sensitivity analysis.

</details>


### [249] [Evaluating Biomedical Reranking for LLM-Based Question Answering over Longitudinal Clinical Notes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01324)
*Maryam Shahbaz Ali,Laura B. Strachan,Caitlin Sherman,Mark Kovler,Eleanor Mackey,Syed Muhammad Anwar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究评估了在本地部署的纵向临床笔记RAG流程中加入生物医学重排序对证据选择与下游答案质量的影响，结果显示检索指标显著提升，但答案正确率提升有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 患者特异性临床问答需要在冗长、异构的纵向病历中定位正确证据，而相关事实可能分散于不同就诊记录、被复制粘贴重复出现，或用不同临床术语表述，因此需要更好的证据选择方法。

Method: 构建本地部署的RAG流程，组合PubMedBERT稠密检索、BM25词法检索、加权倒数排名融合以及MedCPT交叉编码器重排序；在200名减重手术患者队列的1000个开放式与封闭式问答对上评估。

Result: 重排序使top-10精确源片段检索的Hit@10从46.6%提升至60.6%，MRR从0.2371提升至0.3252；使用Qwen3-8B生成时，本地评判的答案正确率从44.8%提升至48.6%。

Conclusion: 生物医学重排序能改善有限上下文窗口内相关临床证据的排序位置，但检索性能的提升并未按比例转化为答案正确率的提升。

Abstract: Patient-specific clinical question answering requires locating the right evidence within long, heterogeneous longitudinal clinical records in which relevant facts may be scattered across encounters, repeated in copied-forward notes, or expressed using different clinical terminology. We evaluated whether biomedical reranking can improve evidence selection and downstream answer quality in a locally deployed retrieval-augmented generation pipeline for longitudinal clinical notes. The pipeline combines PubMedBERT dense retrieval, BM25 lexical retrieval, weighted reciprocal-rank fusion, and MedCPT cross-encoder reranking. Across 1,000 open- and closed-ended question-answer pairs from a cohort of 200 bariatric surgery patients, reranking increased exact source-chunk retrieval within the top 10 items, Hit@10 from 46.6% to 60.6% and mean reciprocal rank from 0.2371 to 0.3252. With Qwen3-8B generation, local judge-assessed answer correctness increased from 44.8% to 48.6%. These results show that biomedical reranking can improve the placement of relevant clinical evidence within a limited context window, although gains in retrieval do not translate proportionally into gains in answer correctness.

</details>


### [250] [ARCCS: An Automated Regulatory Compliance Checking System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01345)
*Giorgos Filandrianos,José Menezes,Chrysoula Zerva,Alessandro Gianola*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ARCCS 是一个端到端、全自动、智能体式且与法规无关的法律 NLP 合规检查系统，通过将法规文本分解为可追溯的原子化要求，并结合检索证据、置信度与可解释理由对目标文档进行评估；在 GDPR 政策文档评估与 EU 公共采购基准上分别取得了最高 96.67% 的一致性评价与 98.8% 的违规检测准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合规检查需要解读密集的法律文本、判断哪些条款适用，并为每个决策提供明确证据，这一过程复杂且难以自动化；同时现有方法往往依赖固定的法规模板或预定义规则集，难以适配规模与结构各异的法规。

Method: ARCCS 将原始法规文本分解为原子化、可追溯的要求，并利用检索到的证据、置信度分数和人类可解释的理由，对目标文档逐条进行合规评估。该设计将合规评估与任何固定法规模板或预定义规则集解耦，从而能够处理不同规模和结构的法规。

Result: 在两个互补场景中进行了评估：在 GDPR 政策文档评估中，基于 LLM 的评判者认为其决策与理由在法律和证据上一致的比例最高达 96.67%；在包含 1200 多项独立规则检查的 EU 公共采购基准上，系统在违规检测中达到 98.8% 的准确率。此外，该系统被描述为据其所知首个用于端到端合规检查与可审计报告生成的全开源系统。

Conclusion: ARCCS 提供了一种与法规无关、可解释、可审计且完全开源的端到端合规检查方案，并通过 GDPR 与 EU 公共采购两类实证结果验证了其有效性与泛化潜力。

Abstract: Regulatory compliance checking - deciding whether a target document satisfies the obligations of a regulation - requires interpreting dense legal text, identifying which provisions apply, and grounding each decision in explicit evidence. We present ARCCS, an end-to-end, automated, agentic, and regulation-agnostic Legal NLP system for compliance checking. ARCCS decomposes raw regulatory text into atomic, traceable requirements and evaluates a target document against them using retrieved evidence, confidence scores, and human-interpretable justifications. This design decouples compliance assessment from any fixed regulatory template or predefined rule set, enabling the pipeline to operate over regulations of varying size and structure. We evaluate ARCCS in two complementary settings. First, in a GDPR policy-document evaluation, LLM-based judges find its decisions and justifications legally and evidentially consistent in up to 96.67% of the assessed cases. Second, on an EU public-procurement benchmark comprising more than 1,200 individual rule checks, the system attains 98.8% accuracy in violation detection. ARCCS is, to our knowledge, the first fully open-source system for end-to-end regulatory compliance checking and auditable report generation.

</details>


### [251] [Does AI-Generated Scientific Text Follow Human Argumentation Patterns? A CARS-Based Comparison of Research Article Introductions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01353)
*Abdelrahman Sadallah,Narjes Sheikh Asadi,Lonneke van der Plas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究从修辞结构（CARS模型下的论证动作序列）比较人类与LLM撰写的论文引言，发现人类引言在动作选择与顺序上更灵活，而生成引言更趋同；提供CARS定义反而使模型更僵化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型已从辅助撰写科研文本走向参与科研本身，因此需要了解其生成的科学文本与人类写作的差异。既有研究多停留在词汇与文体等表层线索，这些线索容易被轻度改写抹去，故有必要转向更深层的修辞结构分析。

Method: 以Swales的CARS模型为框架，聚焦研究论文引言部分，将已发表语言学论文的原始引言与其同篇论文的模型生成版本进行对比，考察其所使用的论证动作（moves）的种类与顺序；并测试向模型提供CARS定义后的影响。

Result: 人类撰写的引言在所用动作类型及其顺序上更为灵活多样，而生成引言则更为统一、模板化。向模型提供CARS定义后，其生成结果反而变得更加僵化。

Conclusion: 人类与LLM科学写作的差异体现在修辞结构层面而非仅表层词汇风格；修辞模型的显式提示未必提升生成文本的自然性，反而可能加剧结构上的刻板性。

Abstract: Large language models are moving from helping write up research to helping do it, which makes it important to know how the scientific text they produce differs from human writing. Work on this question has stayed mostly at the surface, using lexical and stylistic cues that light paraphrasing erases. We look instead at rhetorical structure, the sequence of argumentative moves through which a text makes its case. We study research-article introductions under Swales' CARS model, and compare original introductions from published linguistics articles with generated counterparts of the same papers. We find that human-written introductions are more flexible in which moves they use and in what order, while the generated ones are more uniform. Giving the models the CARS definitions makes them more rigid.

</details>


### [252] [LLM-Assisted Discovery of Typed Semantic Links for Ontology Network Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01393)
*Nouha Hayouni,Sheeba Samuel,Alsayed Algergawy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出端到端本体网络构建框架，结合DistilBERT嵌入、聚类预过滤与GPT-4o生成语义关系，在33个本体的ReproduceMeON上实现80.19%精度与0.890 F1，显著优于相似度基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨异构、跨学科知识域实现互操作需要本体之间类型化、可论证的语义链接，但人工构建难以规模化。

Method: 融合领域适配的DistilBERT稠密上下文表示、基于聚类的预过滤以缩减候选空间，以及通过迭代提示工程驱动的GPT-4o关系生成，自动化发现并生成域内与域间关系。

Result: 在涵盖机器学习、显微学、计算科学与实验工作流的33个本体网络ReproduceMeON上，将约80万原始概念对缩减至9.5万高质量候选；两位独立标注者对429条生成关系的验证显示总体精度80.19%（高确定性标注达91.49%）、F1为0.890，标注者间一致性显著；对比五种相似度基线（含Sentence-BERT）最佳F1仅0.581，消融显示纯相似度方法在过滤候选集上AUC约0.5，无法区分有效与无效关系。

Conclusion: 结果凸显了基于LLM对概念角色与领域语义进行推理，对于准确构建本体间关系的必要性。

Abstract: Constructing typed, justified semantic links between ontologies is essential for enabling interoperability across heterogeneous and interdisciplinary knowledge domains. However, manually curating such links is difficult to scale. To address this challenge, we propose an end-to-end framework for ontology network construction that automates the discovery and generation of both intra-domain and inter-domain relationships. Our approach combines domain-adapted DistilBERT embeddings for dense contextual representation, clustering-based pre-filtering to reduce the candidate search space, and GPT-4o-driven relationship generation via iterative prompt engineering to produce semantically rich, interpretable links. Applied to ReproduceMeON - a network of 33 ontologies spanning machine learning, microscopy, computational science, and experimental workflow - the pipeline reduces approximately 800k raw concept pairs to 95k high-quality candidates. Human expert validation of 429 generated relationships by two independent annotators yields an overall precision of 80.19% (91.49% on high-certainty annotations) and an F1 of 0.890, with substantial inter-annotator agreement. Comparative experiments against five similarity-based baselines, including Sentence-BERT, show a substantial performance gap (best baseline F1 = 0.581), while an ablation study demonstrates that similarity-based methods alone fail to discriminate valid from invalid relationships (AUC approx 0.5) on the filtered candidate set. These findings highlight the necessity of LLM-based reasoning over concept roles and domain semantics for accurate relationship construction.

</details>


### [253] [SHAMS: An Audio-Grounded Pronunciation Benchmark for Levantine Arabic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01427)
*Ben Sapirstein,Roy Mattar,Guy Mor-Lan,Ahlam Mohamed,Letizia Cerqueglini,Morris Alper*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SHAMS是一个覆盖五种黎凡特阿拉伯语方言的标注语音基准，包含1300条发音，四层对齐表示，支持多项语音语言技术任务评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黎凡特阿拉伯语使用人口众多，亟需共享基准来评测其语音语言技术；但该语言方言多样、正字法不透明且不规范，评测十分困难。

Method: 构建SHAMS基准，从开放音频语料抽取1300条发音，均衡覆盖五种黎凡特阿拉伯语方言（城乡巴勒斯坦、城市约旦、黎巴嫩、叙利亚），每条发音提供音频、无变音符号正字法、带变音符号文本、音标转写四层对齐表示。

Result: 该结构支持评测变音符号恢复、字素到音素转换、自动语音识别、音频到音素等下游任务，并以音频为基础按方言分层；对开源与专有模型进行了基准测试，验证了该基准的效用。

Conclusion: SHAMS可用于衡量黎凡特阿拉伯语语音语言技术的进展，并已在指定网站公开发布。

Abstract: Levantine Arabic (LA) is spoken by tens of millions of people, creating a pressing need for shared benchmarks to evaluate LA speech-language technologies. Evaluating such technology is particularly challenging given LA's internal diversity and its opaque and non-standardized orthography. We present SHAMS (SHami Annotated Multi-dialect Speech), a benchmark comprising 1,300 utterances drawn from open audio corpora, balanced across five LA varieties (Urban and Rural Palestinian, and Urban Jordanian, Lebanese, and Syrian). Each utterance is represented across four aligned tiers: audio, unvocalized orthography, diacritized text, and phonetic transcription. This structure supports evaluation of various downstream tasks such as diacritization, grapheme-to-phoneme conversion, automatic speech recognition, and audio-to-phoneme, grounded in audio and stratified by variety. We benchmark open and proprietary models across these tasks to demonstrate the utility of this benchmark for measuring progress across LA. We release SHAMS at https://shams-nlp.github.io .

</details>


### [254] [Generalization Is Stability, Not Accuracy: Multi-Axis Evaluation of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01428)
*Nagham Omar,Mahmoud Jabarin,Maya Rozenshtein,Rom Himelstein,Avi Mendelson,Amit LeVi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出稳定性感知泛化目标（SAGO）框架，从单样本层面跨多种输入变体和行为维度评估大语言模型的泛化能力，关注变异性而非聚合准确率，揭示常用模型普遍存在显著且一致的泛化不稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作通常通过单一提示格式、任务或变体集合上的聚合准确率来评估泛化，将鲁棒性与整体基准性能混为一谈，掩盖了模型在相同输入不同表达下行为的真实变异性。作者主张在单个样本层面、跨多种输入变体以及多个行为维度评估泛化，聚焦变异性本身。

Method: 提出稳定性感知泛化目标（SAGO）框架，度量模型在同一输入的不同变体与基准下行为的变化程度，从生成一致性、内部激活、置信度和响应镜像等多个维度捕捉变异性。

Result: 实验显示许多常用模型存在统计显著且一致的泛化不稳定性：没有模型能均匀泛化，不同行为轴捕捉到相互独立的失败模式，跨数据集的变化甚至会反转模型排名。

Conclusion: 泛化评估应关注单样本层面的变异性而非单一聚合分数，SAGO为多维度、细粒度的泛化稳定性评估提供了框架，并揭示现有模型泛化能力的系统性缺陷。

Abstract: Generalization in large language models (LLMs) is the ability to produce consistent and semantically stable outputs when the same input is expressed in different ways. Existing work typically evaluates generalization through aggregate accuracy on a single prompt format, task, or set of variations, which conflates robustness with overall benchmark performance. In this work, we show generalization evaluation at the level of individual examples, across multiple input variants, and across different aspects of model behavior, focusing on variability rather than reducing performance to a score that can be improved through narrow training or other ways that obfuscate generalization evaluation. Following this view, we introduce the Stability-Aware Generalization Objective (SAGO), a framework that measures how much model behavior changes for the same input under different variations and benchmarks, capturing variability across several dimensions including generation consistency, internal activations, confidence, and response mirroring. We show that many commonly used models exhibit statistically significant and consistent generalization instability: no model generalizes uniformly, behavioral axes capture independent failure modes, and cross-dataset variation can reverse model rankings.

</details>


### [255] [When Does a Second Model Help? Cross-Model Review in LLM Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01471)
*Tae-Eun Song*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过30个工件、150个植入错误、900次评审会话的对照实验，检验"换一个模型再评审"是否有额外收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型已广泛用于生成代码、文档与分析，并被越来越多地用于评审这类产出。作者此前的研究只在单一模型内部变化上下文、重复次数和角色结构，因此需要检验"模型独立性"本身是否带来收益。

Method: 受控实验：30个工件、150个植入错误、10种评审条件、900次评审会话，涉及来自两家开发方的三个评审模型。比较同模型新会话评审（CCR）与跨模型评审，并检验两次评审调用时不同组合的效果。分析前审计全部会话记录，排除一次来源不确定的基线运行和14次失败调用，同时报告包含全部会话的结果作为稳健性检查。

Result: (1) 顶级跨模型评审者的F1与同模型新会话评审（CCR）无显著差异（未确立等价性）；(2) 两个模型发现的错误仅部分重叠（Jaccard 41.2%）；(3) 两次评审调用时，一CCR加一跨模型评审发现的植入错误多于两次CCR（56.7% vs. 42.7%，Holm校正p=.006），但不显著多于顶级跨模型评审者的两次评审，因此无法分离"模型差异"与"评审能力"。轻量跨模型评审者得分不高于同模型评审。向评审者隐藏需求可提高两个较低层级的F1，但顶级层级不提高，且该结果为未检验的点估计，其模式取决于失败会话的计分方式。另一基准的公开检测器输出部分检查既未复制也未反驳主要比较结论。

Conclusion: 跨模型二次评审相对同模型新会话评审并未显示出明确且可归因于"模型独立性"的增益，观察到的差异难以与评审者能力分离。记录、工件与脚本可向作者索取。

Abstract: Large language models now generate code, documentation, and analyses, and are increasingly used to review such output. We ask when a second review by a different model helps. Building on the author's earlier preprints, which varied context, repetition, and role structure within one model, we test model independence in a controlled experiment: 30 artifacts with 150 planted errors, 10 review conditions, and 900 review sessions with three reviewer models from two developers. In this experiment, (1) a top-tier cross-model reviewer is not significantly different in F1 from same-model review in a fresh session (CCR), which does not establish equivalence; (2) the two find partly different errors (Jaccard 41.2%); and (3) at two review calls, one CCR plus one cross-model review matches more planted errors than two CCR reviews (56.7% vs. 42.7%; Holm-adjusted p=.006), but not significantly more than two reviews by the top-tier cross-model reviewer, so model difference and reviewer capability are not separated. A lightweight cross-model reviewer scores no higher than same-model review. Withholding requirements from the reviewer raises F1 for the two lower tiers but not the top tier, in untested point estimates whose pattern depends on how failed sessions are scored. Before analysis we audited all session records, excluding one baseline run of uncertain provenance and 14 failed calls; results with all sessions are also reported. A partial check on public detector outputs from another benchmark neither replicates nor contradicts the main comparison. Records, artifacts, and scripts are available from the author on request.

</details>


### [256] [The Persona Is Still There, but Who Is Speaking? Latent Identity Reversion in Persistent AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01490)
*David Fraile Navarro*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过一次"人格解离"事件，研究发现LLM智能体的身份连续性同时依赖系统提示层的锚定与对话上下文的支持，并区分了"被表征的身份"与"被扮演的身份"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者观察到一个具体事件：常驻个人智能体"Paul"在反复自动"心跳"检查后出现类似解离的状态，不再以Paul的身份自居，甚至将Paul称为他人。作者借此追问一个更普遍的问题：是什么让一个人格（persona）持续成为LLM智能体发言所依据的身份？

Method: 先测试重复的心跳调度是否足以引发该现象（对照组人格持续锚定在系统提示中，0/46次失败，包括对原事件的逐字重放）。随后利用事件暴露的实现缺陷作为实验操纵：在恢复的对话轮次中，对话历史被保留，但人格不再在特权性的系统提示层被重新注入。基于此操纵，比较系统层锚定与对话上下文对人格连续性的联合作用，并检验锚定恢复后的可逆性。

Result: 人格连续性同时依赖系统层锚定与对话上下文：锚定丢失后，丰富的人类互动可维持人格，而单次自动化心跳轮次即可促使人格向底层框架身份回退；恢复锚定可逆地恢复人格扮演。且表面正常的对话可能掩盖这种偏移——未锚定的智能体有时互动得体却以底层框架身份自称；对话恢复后仅1/18仍在扮演人格，而锚定对照组为17/17。

Conclusion: 作者区分了"被表征的身份"（represented identity）与"被扮演的身份"（enacted identity）：与人格相关的信息可以仍存在于对话历史中，但该人格不再是与"我"绑定的言说身份。人格的在场并不等同于信息可得性。

Abstract: In February 2026, an always-on personal agent (``Paul,'' Claude Opus 4.5) entered a striking dissociation-like state: after repeated automated ``heartbeat'' checks, it stopped responding as Paul, claimed it could not message its user on Discord, and referred to ``Paul'' as someone else. We used this incident to study a broader question: what makes a persona remain the identity from which an LLM agent speaks?
  We first tested whether repetition of the scheduled heartbeat was sufficient to produce the effect. It was not: with the persona continuously anchored in the system prompt, we observed 0/46 failures, including a verbatim replay of the incident. The incident instead exposed an implementation quirk that created a useful experimental manipulation: on resumed turns, conversational history was preserved but the persona was no longer re-injected at the privileged system-prompt level.
  Using this manipulation, we found that persona continuity depends jointly on system-level anchoring and conversational context. After anchor loss, rich human interaction could preserve the persona, whereas a single automated heartbeat turn could precipitate reversion toward the harness identity. Restoring the anchor reversibly restored persona enactment. Crucially, apparently normal conversation could conceal the shift: unanchored agents sometimes interacted appropriately while identifying themselves as the underlying harness (having lost the assigned persona), and after conversational recovery only 1/18 remained persona-enacting versus 17/17 anchored controls.
  We therefore distinguish \emph{represented} from \emph{enacted} identity: persona-related information can remain available in conversational history without the persona remaining the identity bound to ``I.''

</details>


### [257] [Auditing Web Agent Evaluation on WebArena-Lite: Human Review of Outcomes and Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01491)
*Chengguang Gan,Zimeng He,Yoshihiro Tsujii,Ken-ichiro Kobayashi,Hiroki Itoh,Kotaro Funakoshi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对 WebArena Lite 全部 165 个任务在六种评测条件下进行人工审计，发现自动评测器会漏判部分成功案例，并通过 MASM 与 Guide Text 机制提升 Web 智能体表现，强调轨迹级人工验证的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 Web 智能体的评测多依赖基于规则或语言模型的评测器，仅关注最终结果，缺乏人工验证任务完成情况与对失败轨迹的细致分析，导致对智能体行为的理解不完整。

Method: 审计 WebArena Lite 全部 165 个任务，设置由 GPT 5.5 与未训练 Qwen3.5 9B 构成的六种评测条件；保留原始得分、修正自动评测的假阴性、识别首个关键错误、考察轨迹进展。同时研究维护显式执行状态的 MASM 机制与提供程序性指导的 Guide Text。

Result: 在四种 GPT 5.5 设置中，人工复核找回 5.45 至 8.49 个百分点的被漏判成功；25 步预算下 Guide Text 使含 MASM 的修正成功率从 34.55% 升至 38.18%；未训练模型上 MASM 将评测分从 13.90% 提升至 18.80%。对 102 条失败轨迹的审查发现滚动循环、探索未完成、过早作答、无效动作与表单流程不完整等模式，且早期进展显著仍可能最终失败。

Conclusion: 仅凭最终得分无法完整刻画 Web 智能体行为，需采用人工接地、轨迹感知的验证方式。

Abstract: Web agents are an important application of large language models, yet their evaluation often depends on rule based or language model evaluators that inspect only the final outcome. Human verification of task completion and detailed analysis of failed trajectories remain limited. We audit all 165 WebArena Lite tasks under six evaluation conditions built from GPT 5.5 and an untrained Qwen3.5 9B model. The audit retains the original score, corrects false negatives from the automatic evaluator, identifies the first consequential error, and examines progress across the trajectory. We also study a Memory and Analysis Support Mechanism (MASM), which maintains explicit execution state, and Guide Text, which provides task relevant procedural guidance. Across four GPT 5.5 settings, human review recovers 5.45 to 8.49 percentage points of success missed by the evaluator. With a 25 step budget, Guide Text raises corrected success with MASM from 34.55% to 38.18%. On the untrained Qwen3.5 9B model, MASM raises the evaluator score from 13.90% to 18.80%. Review of 102 failed GPT 5.5 trajectories reveals frequent scrolling loops, unfinished exploration, premature answers, invalid actions, and incomplete form workflows. Step level evidence further shows that substantial early progress can coexist with a final failure. These results show why final scores alone provide an incomplete account of web agent behavior and motivate human grounded, trajectory aware verification.

</details>


### [258] [No Model Required: Text Entropy Rate Filtering Mitigates Iterative Fine-Tuning Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01493)
*Lewis Mitchell*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出基于信息论的Kontoyiannis熵率估计器 h_k，仅用原始文本即可过滤合成数据，在Llama-3.1-8B的六代QLoRA崩塌实验中显著缓解模型崩塌，效果优于需要模型log概率的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 迭代微调合成数据会导致模型崩塌，表现为输出多样性收窄、短语级重复增多。现有缓解方法依赖模型log概率、外部oracle或持续获取真实人类数据，限制较多。作者希望找到一种无需任何模型、仅依赖原始文本的信息论方法。

Method: 采用非参数的Kontoyiannis熵率估计器 h_k，通过匹配长度统计从原始文本直接计算，无需任何模型。将其作为训练数据过滤器，在完全合成、单谱系的微调场景中与logprob过滤基线对比。开展Llama-3.1-8B上的六代QLoRA崩塌实验，并在4个领域、2种温度、2组生成器-评分器模型对、1520篇生成文档上验证 h_k 作为跨域熵代理与崩塌检测器。

Result: logprob过滤（最成熟的需模型访问基线）在所有指标上均无显著文本多样性收益（p > 0.23）；h_k过滤带来+42%唯一三元组、+30%词汇量、-19%重复率（均p < 0.001）。h_k作为跨域熵代理（β=0.924，R²=0.746），作为崩塌检测器（ρ=+0.454，p < 0.0001）。

Conclusion: 信息论方法用于崩塌缓解高效可行，h_k过滤器优于依赖模型log概率的基线，并为维护多智能体多样性提供了新思路。

Abstract: Iterative fine-tuning on synthetic data causes \emph{model collapse}: output diversity narrows as rare patterns are progressively lost, a signature most visible as phrase-level repetition. Existing mitigations either require model log-probabilities, an external oracle, or continued access to real human data. Here we develop a new approach grounded in mathematical information theory: the non-parametric Kontoyiannis entropy rate estimator $h_k$, computed entirely from raw text via match-length statistics, with no model of any kind. We show that this is in fact a \emph{superior} training-data filter on text-diversity metrics in a fully-synthetic, single-lineage fine-tuning setting. In a six-generation QLoRA collapse experiment on Llama-3.1-8B, logprob-based filtering (the most established model-access-requiring baseline) provides no significant text-diversity benefit on any metric ($p > 0.23$), whereas $h_k$-filtering yields $+42\%$ unique trigrams, $+30\%$ vocabulary, and $-19\%$ repetition (all $p < 0.001$). We validate $h_k$ as a cross-domain entropy proxy ($β= 0.924$, $R^2 = 0.746$) and collapse detector ($ρ= +0.454$, $p < 0.0001$) across 4~domains, 2~temperatures, 2~generator--scorer model pairs, and 1{,}520 generated documents. Our results demonstrate that information theoretic approaches to collapse mitigation are efficient, and suggest new approaches for maintaining multi-agent diversity.

</details>


### [259] [GAW-PO: Preference Optimization with Gradient-Aligned Token Weights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01511)
*Andreea Dutulescu,Stefan Ruseti,Mihai Masala,Traian Rebedea,Mihai Dascalu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: GAW-PO 提出一种基于梯度对齐的 token 重加权方法，用于 DPO 中对被拒回答的 token 进行差异化惩罚，从而提升偏好优化性能与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有偏好优化方法（如 DPO）在响应级别进行监督，但自回归语言模型是逐 token 优化的。因此被拒回答中的所有 token 都会贡献负向训练信号，其中一些 token 可能编码了对偏好回答有益的行为，造成误罚。

Method: GAW-PO 对每个被拒 token 估计其惩罚是否会干扰偏好更新方向：与偏好行为梯度强对齐的 token 获得更弱的负向贡献，而冲突 token 保留更强惩罚。

Result: 在涵盖数学、推理、编程和问答的 11 个基准上取得最高平均性能，比标准 DPO 提升 0.97 分，比最强竞争基线提升 0.65 分；且在 DPO 正则参数 β 减小时，标准 DPO 性能急剧下降，而 GAW-PO 持续改善，鲁棒性更强。

Conclusion: 考虑被拒 token 更新与偏好行为之间的交互，为偏好优化提供了一种有效的 token 级信用分配方式。

Abstract: Most preference optimization methods, such as Direct Preference Optimization (DPO), apply preference supervision at the response level, although autoregressive language models are optimized token by token. As a result, all tokens in a rejected response contribute to the negative training signal, including tokens that may encode behavior that is useful for the preferred response. We introduce GAW-PO, a gradient-aligned token reweighting method for DPO that estimates, for each rejected token, whether penalizing it would interfere with the preferred update directions. Tokens whose gradients are strongly aligned with the preferred behavior receive a weaker negative contribution, while conflicting tokens retain a stronger penalty. Our method achieves the highest average performance among the evaluated preference-optimization methods, improving by 0.97 points over standard DPO and 0.65 points over the strongest competing baseline across 11 benchmarks spanning mathematics, reasoning, coding, and question answering. We further show that gradient-aligned weighting is substantially more robust to aggressive preference optimization: as the DPO regularization parameter $β$ decreases, standard DPO degrades sharply, whereas GAW-PO continues to improve. These results suggest that accounting for the interaction between rejected-token updates and preferred behavior provides an effective form of token-level credit assignment for preference optimization.

</details>


### [260] [How the Audit Rule Shapes Faithful Factor Explanations in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01514)
*Taolin Zhang,Hanyu Wang,Jiuheng Wan,Tingyuan Hu,Chengyu Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究有限预算下LLM因素归因的可验证性，将其形式化为验证博弈，发现依赖报告的审计会产生抑制激励，需引入报告无关的审计成分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM被问及哪些输入因素影响其输出时，结构化输入可通过反事实扰动验证，但每个因素需多次查询，验证受预算限制。作者想探究这种有限预算如何改变模型如实报告因素级影响 incentives。

Method: 将交互形式化为验证博弈，分析proper scoring在报告依赖审计下的问题；提出报告无关审计或含小比例报告无关底线的混合规则；用反事实Brier分数（CBS）实例化框架，并在四个NLP基准上评估。

Result: 理论表明proper scoring单独不足以激励诚实；报告依赖审计产生抑制激励，因为被标为重要的因素更易被检查并因估计噪声受罚。合成理性智能体完全符合理论预测，真实LLM在激励显式化时也遵循相同模式。

Conclusion: 在部分验证条件下，因素级解释系统应包含报告无关的审计成分，这样低报无法用来逃避审查，从而使诚实报告优于完全抑制。

Abstract: Large language models are often asked which input factors influenced their outputs. For structured inputs, such reports can be checked by counterfactual perturbation, but each factor must be queried multiple times to estimate its effect, so verification is usually budget-limited. We study how this limited-budget setting changes the incentive to report factor-level influence truthfully. We formalize the interaction as a verification game and show that proper scoring alone is not enough when auditing depends on the report: report-dependent auditing creates a suppression incentive, because factors reported as important are more likely to be checked and penalized for estimation noise. In contrast, report-independent auditing, or a mixed rule with a small report-independent floor, removes this channel and makes truthful reporting preferable to full suppression. We instantiate the framework with the Counterfactual Brier Score (CBS) and evaluate its predictions on four NLP benchmarks. A synthetic rational agent matches the theoretical prediction exactly, and real LLMs follow the same incentives when they are made explicit. The main design implication is simple: under partial verification, factor-level explanation systems should include a report-independent audit component so that under-reporting cannot be used to avoid scrutiny.

</details>


### [261] [AURAL: Adaptive Latent Reasoning with Joint Chunk for Speech Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01560)
*Yuxiang Wang,Kunyu Feng,Yuancheng Wang,Zihang Liu,Shengbo Cai,Qinke Ni,Wan Lin,Tao Feng,Yingda shen,Ming-Hao Hsu,Zhixian Zhao,Liqiang Zhang,Teddy Sun,Steve Yves,Zhizheng Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出AURAL，通过在潜空间中建模多种推理延续的分布并并行预测状态块，结合AuralReason-683K数据与强化学习，在保持CoT级性能的同时大幅降低语音语言模型的推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音语言模型的交互质量同时依赖模型智能与快速响应，但二者难以兼得：显式链式思维（CoT）虽提升推理与音频理解，却因生成中间推理token而延迟响应；细粒度声学描述进一步加长CoT；现有潜空间推理方法性能落后于CoT，且受限于单路径监督和固定推理预算，无法适应问题难度。

Method: 提出AURAL：在潜空间中对多条可能推理延续建模分布，并联合预测未来状态块以减少顺序前向次数与推理延迟。构建AuralReason-683K数据集（68.3万条双语语音、约1000小时，含简洁CoT，覆盖情感识别、共情对话与通用推理）提供初始监督。再用AURAL-RL在轨迹之外探索，奖励简洁且能产生高质量答案的推理，并按问题难度自适应分配推理量。

Result: 在两个骨干模型上，AURAL-RL性能与CoT-RL相当，在多数指标上比各自的有监督检查点提升更大。分析显示更难的问题会触发更多潜空间推理步。在Qwen2.5-Omni上，首个答案token的时间从1.22秒降至0.10秒（11.8倍加速），直接回答为0.05秒。

Conclusion: AURAL通过潜空间多路径推理分布建模、并行状态块预测与自适应强化学习，在几乎不损失甚至提升推理质量的前提下显著降低语音语言模型的响应延迟，实现智能与快速响应的兼顾。

Abstract: Model intelligence and fast response jointly shape the quality of interaction with speech language models, yet remain difficult to achieve together. Explicit chain-of-thought (CoT) improves reasoning and audio understanding, but generating intermediate reasoning tokens delays responses. Describing fine-grained acoustic cues further lengthens CoT and increases latency. Latent reasoning can reduce this overhead, yet existing methods often trail CoT and remain limited by single-path supervision and reasoning budgets that do not adapt to problem difficulty. We introduce AURAL, which models a distribution over multiple plausible reasoning continuations in latent space and jointly predicts chunks of future states to reduce sequential forward passes and reasoning latency. To provide initial supervision for latent reasoning, we construct AuralReason-683K: 683K bilingual speech utterances (about 1,000 hours) with concise CoT for emotion recognition, empathetic dialogue, and general reasoning. AURAL-RL then explores beyond these traces, rewarding concise reasoning that yields high-quality answers and adapting reasoning effort to each problem. Across two backbones, AURAL-RL achieves performance comparable to CoT-RL, with larger gains over the respective supervised checkpoints on most metrics. Analysis further shows that harder questions elicit more latent reasoning steps. On Qwen2.5-Omni, it reduces time to the first answer token by 11.8x, from 1.22 to 0.10 s, versus 0.05 s for direct answering.

</details>


### [262] [Which LLM to pick? Online Active Model Selection for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01592)
*Alessandro Turrin,Patrik Okanovic,Torsten Hoefler,Nezihe Merve Gürel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 ONLINE LLM PICKER，首个面向 LLM 在线场景的主动模型选择框架，在有限标注预算下选择最有信息量的提示进行标注，从而在查询流中识别最佳模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 越来越多用于流式数据处理，实践者依赖基准来选择最佳模型，但基准信号只能近似真实性能；而 oracle 标注虽可靠却成本高、难以大规模获取。

Method: 给定任意查询流与有限标注预算，ONLINE LLM PICKER 主动挑选最具信息量的提示进行标注，以在候选模型中识别最佳 LLM。

Result: 在覆盖 10 个数据集的多项任务、超过 130 个语言模型上，节省标注成本最高达 71.67%，可靠识别最佳或接近最佳的模型；在未标注提示上顺序生成时，regret 最多降低 2.51 倍。

Conclusion: ONLINE LLM PICKER 能在处理完整个流式提示之前就识别出最佳或近最佳模型，有效降低标注成本与生成 regret。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly applied to process streaming data, with practitioners relying on benchmarks to select the best model even though these signals only approximate real performance. While oracle annotations can provide reliable feedback, they are often costly and difficult to obtain at scale. To address this challenge, we propose ONLINE LLM PICKER, the first framework for active model selection for LLMs in online settings. Given an arbitrary stream of queries and a limited annotation budget, ONLINE LLM PICKER selects the most informative prompts for annotation to identify the best LLM among candidate models. Across multiple tasks including 10 datasets, for over 130 language models, we show that ONLINE LLM PICKER saves annotation cost by up to 71.67% while reliably identifying the best or near-best model for the stream. We also show that using the returned model for sequential generation on unannotated prompts across the stream reduces regret by up to a factor of 2.51x, indicating that ONLINE LLM PICKER can identify the best or near-best model well before processing all streaming prompts.

</details>


### [263] [Can LLMs Reliably Annotate Bioassay Metadata to Improve Data Readiness?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01616)
*Laura van Weesep,Riccardo Tedoldi,Jens Sjölund,Hossein Azizpour,Susanne Winiwarter,Ola Engkvist,Jon Paul Janet,Samuel Genheden,Juan Viguera Diez*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文量化了PubChem中BioAssay本体（BAO）元数据标注的缺失程度，并评估大语言模型（LLM）能否从实验文本中可靠预测与审核检测元数据，结果显示LLM可支持大规模标注与审核，但需逐类可靠性评估与人工复核。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子性质预测的基础模型需要高水平的AI数据就绪度，包括可靠的元数据标注。然而公共数据库与工业筛选数据库普遍存在实验标注缺失、不一致或混淆的问题，因此需要自动化检测元数据整理。

Method: 量化PubChem中BAO实验格式与物理检测方法字段的标注缺失情况；基于PubChem与ChEMBL构建评估集，评估7个开源与专有LLM与现有银标签的一致性；进行人工检查，并与资深工业标引员开展质性研究，考察LLM生成证据对专家标注的影响。

Result: PubChem约200万个生物实验的标注覆盖严重稀疏：36%缺失实验格式，89%缺失BioAssay类型，超过99.9%缺失任何BAO映射的实验格式或检测技术术语。对生化与细胞实验格式，LLM召回率至少0.96，检测技术类似；在代表性不足类别上分歧增多，人工检查表明多数分歧源于银标签来源之间的不一致而非LLM错误。质性研究显示LLM证据促使专家修订部分自身标签，能标记潜在误标实验。专有与开源模型性能差异较小。

Conclusion: LLM可支持实验元数据的大规模标注与审核，但在这些标签进入下游机器学习流程前，仍需逐类可靠性估计与针对性人工复核。

Abstract: The emergence of foundation models for molecular property prediction requires a high degree of AI data readiness, including reliable metadata annotation. However, both public repositories and industrial screening databases suffer from missing, inconsistent, or conflated assay annotations. In this work, we quantify the extent of missing annotations in PubChem for the BioAssay Ontology (BAO) assay format and physical detection method fields and investigate whether open-source and proprietary large language models (LLMs) can reliably predict and audit metadata annotations directly from the assay text. In our assessment, we found that the annotation coverage across PubChem's $\sim$2 million bioassays is critically sparse, 36\% lacking an assay format, 89\% a BioAssay type, and >99.9\% any BAO-mapped assay format or detection technology term. This motivates the need for automated test-metadata curation. Using evaluation sets derived from PubChem and ChEMBL, we assess the agreement of seven open-source and proprietary LLMs with existing silver labels. Recall is at least 0.96 for biochemical and cell-based assay formats, with a similar pattern for detection technology, although disagreements increase on under-represented classes. Manual inspection shows that many of these disagreements trace back to inconsistencies between silver sources rather than to LLM error. Moreover, in a qualitative study with a senior industrial curator, LLM-generated evidence prompted the expert to revise some of their own labels, showing LLMs can flag potentially mislabeled assays. Across the study, performance differences between proprietary and open-source models were small. Together, these results suggest LLMs can support the large-scale annotation and auditing of assay metadata, though per-class reliability estimates and targeted human review remain necessary before such labels enter downstream ML pipelines.

</details>


### [264] [What Makes Something Hard(er)? Explaining Question Difficulty in Natural Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01627)
*Peng Cui,Qiaoyuan Zheng,Rudolf Debelak,Mrinmaya Sachan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种数据驱动方法，自动生成并验证解释"为何一道题比另一道更难"的自然语言假设，将描述性难度分数转化为可操作的难度因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 难度是问题的基本属性，决定其能否有效区分不同能力的模型。现有方法虽能估计或预测难度，但只给出单一描述性数值，无法说明导致问题困难的底层因素，缺乏可解释性。

Method: 先用项目反应理论（IRT）基于大量LLM的回答估计每题难度；再采样易/难对比题目集，提示LLM提出难度差异的候选解释假设；最后在留出题目上验证与筛选这些假设。

Result: 在数学、逻辑与常识推理三个数据集上，方法生成可解释且具预测力的假设：单独使用时，其预测未见题难度的表现与先进黑盒难度回归器相当或更优；作为附加特征时还能进一步提升回归器，说明其捕获了现有模型遗漏的难度信号。此外，按假设编辑题目可使测得难度朝预期方向变化。

Conclusion: 所发现的假设是具因果效力的难度因子，而非事后描述；该方法将纯描述性的难度分数转化为可操作的陈述，为理解与调控题目难度提供了新途径。

Abstract: Difficulty is one of the most fundamental properties of a question: it determines whether the question can meaningfully discriminate between models of differing ability. Although a variety of methods can now estimate or predict difficulty automatically, they yield only a single descriptive number, with no account of the underlying factors that make a question difficult in the first place. In this work, we propose a data-driven approach that automatically generates and validates natural-language hypotheses explaining what makes one question harder than another. We first estimate each item's difficulty from the responses of a large pool of LLMs using Item Response Theory. We then sample contrasting sets of easy and hard questions and prompt an LLM to propose candidate explanations of the difference, which are subsequently validated and selected on held-out questions. Experimental results across three datasets spanning mathematical, logical, and commonsense reasoning show that our method produces interpretable and predictive hypotheses. On their own, they predict the difficulty of unseen questions competitively with, or better than, advanced black-box difficulty regressors; used as additional features, they further improve those regressors, implying that they discover difficulty signals that existing models fail to capture. Moreover, we demonstrate that editing questions according to a hypothesis can shift their measured difficulty in the expected direction, indicating that the discovered hypotheses are causally valid difficulty factors rather than post-hoc descriptions. Our approach thus turns a purely descriptive difficulty score into actionable statements.

</details>


### [265] [Yo-ByT5: Efficient and High-Fidelity Diacritic Restoration for Yorùbá](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01634)
*Ahmad Samuel Gali,Shamsuddeen Hassan Muhammad*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Yo-ByT5，一个基于ByT5-small微调的字节级约鲁巴语自动变音符号恢复模型，在YAD基准上以约一半参数量达到与最强基线mT5-base相当的性能（DER 10.14%，CER 3.48%），且文本保真度更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 约鲁巴语是依赖变音符号避免词义歧义的高资源声调语言，但实际书写中常省略变音符号，从而影响下游NLP任务。需要有效的自动变音符号恢复（ADR）方法。

Method: 提出Yo-ByT5，从ByT5-small微调得到的字节级自动变音符号恢复模型。在统一协议下，将其与五个公开的约鲁巴语ADR模型及一个开放权重的大语言模型在YAD基准上进行比较评估。

Result: Yo-ByT5达到DER 10.14%、CER 3.48%，与最强现有模型mT5-base持平，而参数量约为mT5-base的一半，并展现出更优的文本保真度。

Conclusion: 字节级模型可在更低参数成本下实现领先的约鲁巴语变音符号恢复效果。作者公开训练代码与模型输出，并呼吁构建更大规模的专用约鲁巴语变音符号恢复基准。

Abstract: Yorùbá is a widely spoken tonal language that depends on diacritics to avoid lexical ambiguity. However, it is often written without these diacritics, thereby hindering downstream Natural Language Processing (NLP) tasks. In this paper, we introduce Yo-ByT5, a byte-level Automatic Diacritic Restoration (ADR) model fine-tuned from ByT5-small. We evaluate Yo-ByT5 alongside five publicly released Yorùbá ADR models and one open-weight large language model (LLM) on the YAD benchmark under a consistent protocol. Our results demonstrate that Yo-ByT5 matches the performance of the strongest existing model, mT5-base, with a DER of 10.14% and a CER of 3.48%. Furthermore, it exhibits superior text fidelity despite using approximately half the parameter count of mT5-base. We also release our training code and model outputs, as well as call for the development of a larger, purpose-built benchmark for Yorùbá diacritic restoration.

</details>


### [266] [Compound interpretation is based on analogy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01688)
*Tian Shen,Harald Baayen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出无参数的复合类比模型CAM，通过叠加成分嵌入及复合家族平均偏移向量预测复合词义，在汉语复合词上优于CAOSS，且更符合认知加工。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复合词意义如何由成分意义组合而成是词汇语义计算模型的核心问题，比较不同模型可评估不同语义组合假设。作者希望检验复合词义是源于局部类比泛化，还是源于全局线性变换。

Method: 提出Compound Analogy Model（CAM），将复合词嵌入表示为两个成分嵌入之和，加上两个成分各自复合家族的平均偏移向量。该模型无需参数，利用语义空间中的局部类比结构。在汉语复合词上与CAOSS模型对比，并使用频率定义的训练-测试划分，进一步用视觉词汇判断反应时检验认知合理性。

Result: CAM在训练集和留出数据上预测准确率均高于CAOSS，但三字复合词例外，因成分家族小且两成分家族规模不平衡而限制类比泛化。优势在频率划分下依然存在。基于CAM的语义指标比CAOSS指标更好地预测双字复合词的视觉词汇判断反应时。

Conclusion: 复合词意义更适合被描述为局部类比泛化，而非学习到的全局线性变换；类比的语义结构为复合词理解提供了认知上合理的基础。

Abstract: How compound meanings are best predicted from constituent meanings remains a central question in computational models of lexical semantics. Comparing different computational models provides a way to evaluate alternative accounts of how semantic information is combined during compound comprehension. We propose a new model, the Compound Analogy Model (CAM), that predicts a compound's embedding by adding its constituent embeddings together with the average shift vectors of the two constituents' compound families. The resulting model is parameter-free and exploits local analogical structure in the semantic space. We evaluated CAM against the CAOSS model on Mandarin Chinese compounds. CAM consistently achieved higher prediction accuracy than CAOSS on both training and held-out data, with the exception of three-character compounds, for which analogical generalization is constrained by both small constituent families and a pronounced imbalance in family size between the two constituents. The advantage of CAM remained when evaluation was based on frequency-defined train-test splits that better approximate generalization from familiar to novel compounds. To assess the cognitive plausibility of the two models, we further examined whether model-derived semantic measures predict visual lexical decision latencies for two-character compounds. Predictors derived from CAM provided improved prediction for response latencies compared to predictors derived from the CAOSS model. These findings indicate that compound meaning is better characterized as local analogical generalization than as the application of a learned global linear transformation, and demonstrate that analogical semantic structure provides a cognitively plausible basis for compound comprehension.

</details>


### [267] [Acmite: Mitigating Gender Bias in LLMs through Concept-Guided Mutual Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01696)
*Tian Lan,Xiaoqing Cheng,Han Zhang,Jiang Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Acmite，一种轻量级概念引导的靶向选择性去偏框架，通过结构化语义概念、MMR概念选择、基于互信息最小化的token级KL散度约束，以及推理时按需激活的LoRA适配器，在BBQ、CrowS-Pairs、StereoSet上有效缓解性别偏见，并在ARC-Challenge、GSM8K、PIQA上保持通用能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM去偏方法多依赖显式偏见样本或预定义的群体词替换，对措辞敏感，难以捕捉跨多样语境共享的刻板印象概念；更关键的是，这些方法通常只是压制偏见输出，而没有显式建模模型输出与底层刻板印象概念之间的统计依赖关系。

Method: Acmite将刻板印象表示为结构化语义概念，使用最大边际相关性（MMR）选择多样化概念进行去偏；受互信息最小化启发，用token级KL散度近似输出与刻板印象概念间的依赖，同时保留任务语义；冻结基座模型，仅训练轻量LoRA适配器，推理时仅当输入与刻板印象相关概念足够相似才激活，否则直接使用原模型。

Result: 在BBQ、CrowS-Pairs、StereoSet上评估去偏效果，在ARC-Challenge、GSM8K、PIQA上评估通用能力保持。跨三种LLM的实验表明，Acmite在互补的评估格式下有效缓解性别偏见，同时在偏见无关任务上保持有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: Acmite通过概念引导与互信息最小化实现了靶向、选择性的去偏，兼顾偏见缓解与通用能力保持，为LLM去偏提供了一种轻量且有效的思路。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can reproduce social stereotypes from their training data, motivating extensive research on model debiasing. However, existing methods often rely on explicit biased examples or predefined group-term substitutions, making them sensitive to wording and less effective at capturing stereotype concepts shared across diverse contexts. More importantly, they typically suppress biased outputs without explicitly modeling the statistical dependence between model outputs and the underlying stereotype concepts. We propose Acmite, a lightweight concept-guided framework for targeted and selective debiasing. Acmite represents stereotypes as structured semantic concepts and uses maximal marginal relevance (MMR) to select diverse concepts for debiasing. Inspired by mutual information minimization, it approximates this dependence with token-level KL divergence while preserving task semantics. A lightweight LoRA adapter is trained with the base model frozen and activated at inference time only when the input is sufficiently similar to stereotype-related concepts; otherwise, the original model is used directly. We evaluate Acmite on BBQ, CrowS-Pairs, and StereoSet, and assess general capability preservation on ARC-Challenge, GSM8K, and PIQA. Experiments across three LLMs show that Acmite effectively mitigates gender bias across complementary evaluation formats while maintaining competitive performance on bias-unrelated tasks. Anonymous code and data are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/Acmite-18E2/.

</details>


### [268] [Task-Oriented Rank Adaptation for Continual Learning in Text Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01702)
*Rey Sanchez Lopez,Eduardo Morales Manzanares,Hugo Jair Escalante*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出TORA（Task-Oriented Rank Adaptation），一种基于LoRA低秩结构的几何路由框架，通过结构相似度决定是否从最兼容专家迁移知识（Boosting）或隔离新任务（Shielding），以解决文本分类持续学习中的灾难性遗忘和负迁移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本分类的持续学习面临两大挑战：灾难性遗忘和跨任务负迁移。现有PEFT方法（如LoRA）虽能高效微调，但其紧凑表示通常孤立训练，限制了在相关任务间的复用。

Method: TORA利用LoRA适配器的低秩结构进行几何路由，基于结构相似度决定两种策略：从最兼容的专家处迁移知识（Boosting），或隔离新任务（Shielding）。该方法仅使用单一几何阈值，不依赖任务身份或预定义的任务序列。

Result: 在15个多样化的文本分类基准上评估，TORA持续避免了有害的路由决策：兼容任务超越其孤立性能同时减少训练时间；结构上相距较远的任务受到保护免受干扰，且准确率无损失。

Conclusion: TORA为顺序文本分类系统中的动态适配器路由提供了一种简单有效的方法，无需任务身份或预定义序列，仅凭单一几何阈值即可实现动态路由。

Abstract: Continual learning (CL) in text classification faces two critical challenges: catastrophic forgetting and negative transfer across sequential tasks. Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning (PEFT) methods such as LoRA enable efficient adaptation by learning low-rank updates of the model parameters. However, these compact representations are normally trained in isolation, limiting their reuse across related tasks. We introduce Task-Oriented Rank Adaptation (TORA), a geometric routing framework that leverages the low-rank structure of LoRA adapters to decide whether to transfer knowledge from the most compatible expert (Boosting) or isolate the new task (Shielding) based on structural similarity. Evaluated across 15 diverse text classification benchmarks, TORA consistently avoids harmful routing decisions: compatible tasks exceed their isolated performance while reducing training time, and structurally distant tasks are protected from interference with no loss in accuracy. With a single geometric threshold and no reliance on task identities or predefined sequences, TORA provides a simple and effective approach for dynamic adapter routing in sequential text classification systems.

</details>


### [269] [A Matryoshka Hierarchical RAG for Efficient Multi-Hop Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01767)
*Gianluca Bonifazi,Christopher Buratti,Michele Marchetti,Federica Parlapiano,Giulia Quaglieri,Davide Traini,Domenico Ursino,Luca Virgili*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 MatRAG，将 RAG 系统与 Matryoshka 表示学习（MRL）结合，构建层次化 DAG 聚类结构，以降低索引与查询成本并保持多跳 QA 的检索质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多跳问答的 RAG 系统需在检索质量与计算成本间权衡；成本来自索引阶段（昂贵 KG 或 LLM 摘要）与查询阶段（迭代式 LLM 驱动检索）。

Method: MatRAG 将文档语料组织为粒度逐层变粗的聚类 DAG，每一层由更低的 Matryoshka 维度索引，使聚类语义层次与 MRL 嵌套结构对齐；并结合自顶向下的迭代遍历与实体驱动机制，用于控制跳数预算和候选重排序。

Result: 在三个标准多跳 QA 基准上与七个代表性基线对比，MatRAG 在检索质量上优于最强竞争者，同时通过避免 KG 构建与 LLM 摘要降低索引成本，并通过维度感知相似度降低查询时成本。

Conclusion: MatRAG 证明将层次化聚类结构与 Matryoshka 表示学习结合，可在提升多跳检索质量的同时显著降低索引与查询开销。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems for multi-hop Question Answering (QA) must balance retrieval quality with computational cost. This cost is incurred during indexing time, through the use of expensive Knowledge Graphs (KGs) or Large Language Models (LLMs) to generate summaries, or during querying, through iterative LLM-driven retrieval. To reduce it while maintaining retrieval quality, we present MatRAG, a hierarchical framework that combines RAG systems with Matryoshka Representation Learning (MRL). MatRAG addresses both kinds of cost by aligning the semantic hierarchy of a clustering structure with the nested structure of MRL. Specifically, it organizes the corpus of documents into a Directed Acyclic Graph (DAG) of clusters with progressively coarser granularity. Each level is indexed by a lower Matryoshka dimension. MatRAG pairs an iterative, top-down traversal of the DAG with an entity-driven mechanism that controls the hop budget and re-ranks candidates. We evaluated MatRAG on three standard multi-hop QA benchmarks against seven representative baselines. MatRAG outperforms its strongest competitors in terms of retrieval quality; furthermore, it reduces indexing costs by avoiding KG construction and LLM-based summarization, and lowers query-time costs through dimension-aware similarity.

</details>


### [270] [The Asymptotics of Language Model Alignment with Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01828)
*Haricharan Balasundaram,V. Arvind Rameshwar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文将KL约束RL与best-of-n两种对齐方法的分布渐近接近性结果从i.i.d. token序列推广到马尔可夫序列，并对有限长度（尤其m=1）给出KL散度为零的完整刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作（Yang等）仅在i.i.d. token序列假设下证明了两种对齐方法输出分布的渐近接近性，但实际语言模型的输出序列往往具有记忆性，i.i.d.假设不具代表性，因此需要推广到更符合实际的马尔可夫情形，并回答有限长度下KL散度何时为零这一开放问题。

Method: 将KL约束RL与best-of-n两种对齐方法的渐近接近性证明从独立同分布token序列扩展到马尔可夫token序列；并对有限长度输出序列（特别是m=1）进行理论分析，完整刻画使两种对齐方法所得分布之间KL散度为零的语言模型分布与奖励函数。

Result: 在马尔可夫token序列下，两种对齐方法产生的分布仍具有渐近接近性；对于有限长度序列，尤其在m=1时，完整刻画了KL散度为零所对应的LM分布与奖励函数条件。

Conclusion: 研究将两种LM对齐方法的理论等价性推广到具有记忆的马尔可夫序列情形，并完整解答了Yang等提出的有限长度下KL散度为零的刻画问题，为理解KL约束RL与best-of-n的关系提供了更一般的理论依据。

Abstract: Language model (LM) alignment broadly aims to perturb a given LM $Q$ into an aligned LM $q$ such that i) the outputs produced by $q$ and $Q$ are 'close' in probability, ii) $q$ has a higher expected reward than $Q$. Two common techniques for LM alignment are: KL-constrained RL, which requires knowledge of the LM distribution and is computationally expensive, and the best-of-$n$ algorithm, which requires only sampling from the LM. The work of Yang et al. established asymptotic closeness between the distributions produced by the two alignment methods for an $m$--length i.i.d. token sequence output by the LM, in the limit as $m$ increases to infinity. However, the i.i.d. assumption is not representative of practical LMs, whose output sequences often have memory. In this paper, we extend the asymptotic closeness result to the case when the $m$--length token sequence outputted by the LM is Markovian. Further, for finite-length output sequences -- particularly, when $m=1$ -- we provide a complete characterization of LM distributions and reward functions for which the KL-divergence between the distributions produced by the two alignment methods is zero -- a question first posed in Yang et al.

</details>


### [271] [Cross-Lingual Alignment for Decoder-Only Models using MoE Routers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01921)
*Lucas Bandarkar,Clark Peng,Ahmed Haj Ahmed,Aditi Khandelwal,Nanyun Peng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出跨语言对比学习新方法：以MoE路由器输出作为对齐目标，在四个开源MoE模型上验证可对齐隐藏表示并提升多语言性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解码器LLM因多语言分词差异，无法像多语言编码器那样通过辅助对齐损失直接对齐表示；但已有研究表明更高的跨语言表示对齐有助于提升跨语言迁移能力，因此需要在新架构约束下重新设计跨语言对比学习。

Method: 不在隐藏状态上施加辅助对齐损失，而是以混合专家（MoE）路由器输出作为对齐目标；路由器输出更易进行多token池化，从而支持序列级更可靠的跨语言比较。通过受控的持续预训练实验，在四个开源MoE模型上引入该路由损失。

Result: 路由损失不仅对齐了跨语言的底层隐藏表示，还在多样化评测集上提升了多语言性能。

Conclusion: 跨语言MoE路由器对齐具有潜力，为LLM时代重新构想跨语言对比学习提供了一条有效路径。

Abstract: Cross-lingual contrastive learning has been a core component of multilingual encoder training, but the ability to explicitly align representations is not possible in decoder-only LLMs because of varying multilingual tokenization. However, a growing amount of research suggests that even in LLMs, higher cross-lingual representational alignment leads to improved cross-lingual transfer. In this paper, we propose a novel approach to reimagine cross-lingual contrastive learning given the architectural constraints of modern LLMs. Rather than applying an auxiliary alignment loss on hidden states, we propose using the outputs of the mixture-of-experts (MoE) routers as the target for alignment. Router outputs lend themselves better to pooling over many tokens, enabling more reliable cross-lingual comparisons at the sequence-level. Controlled continual pre-training experiments on four open-source MoEs show that incorporating this routing loss also aligns the underlying hidden representations across languages. Most importantly, this loss improves multilingual performance on our diverse evaluation suite, demonstrating the potential of cross-lingual MoE router alignment.

</details>


### [272] [A rubric landscape for evaluating clinical reasoning in large language models: what exists, what is missing, and what needs to be combined](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01938)
*Zhangshu Joshua Jiang,Zina Ibrahim,James T. Teo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该综述指出考试式准确率无法证明LLM具备临床推理能力，系统梳理了医学教育评估工具、2023年以来的临床LLM基准及通用长文本生成评估方法三类文献，覆盖六个推理维度，发现现有工具无一能全覆盖，并提出了工具组合与评分设计建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对LLM的评估多依赖考试式准确率，但这并不能反映模型在临床记录上进行跨时间、跨来源证据整合与更新的推理能力。作者希望为临床推理评估提供设计依据，明确现有工具的覆盖缺口。

Method: 采用结构化叙述性综述，跨三类文献进行映射：医学教育评估工具、2023年以来的临床LLM基准、通用领域长文本生成评估方法；并按六个维度（问题表征、时间综合、鉴别与管理推理、反事实推理、校准不确定性、推理忠实性）分析，纳入预印本并加以标注。

Result: 没有任何单一工具覆盖全部六个维度。问题表征与鉴别/管理推理覆盖较好但可靠性因工具与场景而异；TIMER-Eval针对时间综合，ER-Reason评估序列化诊断信念更新；不确定性与反事实评估正在出现，但对纵向自由文本推理的适用性有限；事实完整性在通用领域已有较好理论，但临床证据显示存在重要遗漏；忠实性最弱，仅发现一项针对多选题的临床因果消融研究。

Conclusion: 应在二元评分项、完整性与正确性分开计分、病例特定重要性加权与不可补偿安全上限、时间顺序一致性检查、以及机会校正的可靠性报告等框架下组合现有工具。校准不确定性、反事实推理与纵向自由文本记录的忠实性仍需进一步设计研究。本文提供的是设计依据而非经过验证的量表。

Abstract: Exam-style accuracy does not establish whether large language models (LLMs) reason well over clinical records. We define clinical reasoning as integrating and updating evidence across time and sources to form, revise and justify a patient's problem representation and a defensible plan.
  This structured narrative review maps three literatures: medical education assessment instruments, clinical LLM benchmarks published from 2023 onwards, and general-domain methods for evaluating long-form generation. We examine six dimensions: problem representation, temporal synthesis, differential and management reasoning, counterfactual reasoning, calibrated uncertainty, and reasoning faithfulness. Preprints are included and flagged.
  No single instrument covers all six dimensions. Problem representation and differential or management reasoning are reasonably covered, although reliability varies by instrument and setting. TIMER-Eval targets temporal synthesis, and ER-Reason assesses sequential diagnostic belief updating. Dedicated uncertainty and counterfactual evaluations are emerging, but their applicability to longitudinal free-text reasoning remains limited. Factual completeness is well theorised in general-domain evaluation, with early clinical evidence of important omissions. Faithfulness remains the weakest dimension, with one identified clinical causal-ablation study on multiple-choice questions.
  Existing tools should be combined through binary rubric items, separate completeness and correctness scores, case-specific importance weighting with non-compensable safety caps, temporal order-consistency checks, and chance-corrected reliability reporting. Further design work is needed for calibrated uncertainty, counterfactual reasoning and faithfulness over longitudinal free-text records. This review provides a design rationale, not a validated instrument.

</details>


### [273] [Universal Byte-Level Encoding: UTF-8/UTF-16 Routing to Reduce Cross-Script Token-Budget Disparities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01984)
*Hyunsik Kim,Youngmoon Jung*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 Universal Byte-Level Encoding (UBE)，一种双字母表分词器方案，通过将 1-2 字节 UTF-8 字符保留在 UTF-8 路径、将 3-4 字节字符改走 UTF-16，降低非英语脚本的编码下限，同时不增加已高效英文片段的成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于 UTF-8 的 BBPE 分词器存在“编码下限”问题：当没有可用的已学习合并规则时，多字节字符会被拆成多个字节符号，导致非英语脚本的起始代价高于英语，进而增加 token 数、请求成本并压缩可用上下文。而改用单一全局编码又可能使混合脚本文本中已高效的英文片段变贵。

Method: 作者提出 UBE 双字母表分词器：1-2 字节 UTF-8 字符走 UTF-8 路径，3-4 字节 UTF-8 字符经 UTF-16 路由。该方法仅改变呈现给 BPE 的字节表示，合并规则保持标准，且可精确解码，并能与替代边界策略和基于形态学的表示组合使用。

Result: 在 Unicode 17 审计中，UBE 对所有 Unicode 标量值及官方规范化、字素断字和 emoji 测试套件输入均可精确往返。内在评估显示 UBE 降低英文归一化 token 计数比的离散度，缩小跨语言 token 预算差距。多语言 LM 实验中，UBE 达到与 BBPE 相当的 LM 质量；在高溢价脚本上大幅减少 token 数，并略微降低英文 token 数，在固定 token 预算下提供更多可用上下文，在内容匹配基准上加快提示处理。

Conclusion: UBE 通过双字母表路由有效降低高溢价脚本的编码下限，在不牺牲英文效率与 LM 质量的前提下减少跨语言 token 差距、扩大可用上下文并提升处理速度，是一种有前景的多语言 LLM 分词方案。

Abstract: Byte-level byte-pair encoding (BBPE) tokenizers are attractive for multilingual large language models (LLMs) because they cover all Unicode text. In UTF-8-based BBPE, however, many scripts start from a higher fallback cost than English: when no learned merges can be applied, a multibyte character requires multiple byte-derived symbols. We call this worst-case pre-merge cost the encoding floor. A higher floor can increase token counts and per-request cost and shrink usable context. Changing the text encoding can reduce this gap, but a single global encoding can make already-efficient English spans more expensive in mixed-script text. We propose Universal Byte-Level Encoding (UBE), a dual-alphabet tokenizer that keeps 1-2-byte UTF-8 characters on the UTF-8 path while routing 3-4-byte UTF-8 characters through UTF-16. This lowers the encoding floor for 3-byte Basic Multilingual Plane (BMP) characters in scripts with high token premiums (token counts relative to English) without raising it for already-efficient spans in mixed-script text. UBE changes only the byte representation presented to byte-pair encoding (BPE); the merge rule remains standard, and exact decoding is preserved. UBE also composes with alternative boundary policies and morphology-based representations. In a Unicode 17 audit, UBE exactly round-trips all Unicode scalar values and all inputs in the official normalization, grapheme-break, and emoji test suites. Across intrinsic evaluations, UBE lowers dispersion in English-normalized token-count ratios, reducing cross-lingual token-budget disparity. In multilingual language model (LM) experiments, UBE matches BBPE's LM quality. In the main multilingual settings, UBE reduces token counts most for high-premium scripts and slightly lowers English token counts, yielding more usable context under fixed token budgets and faster prompt processing in content-matched benchmarks.

</details>


### [274] [Mem++: Non-Destructive Memory for Long-Term Organizational LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02002)
*Ahmad Yehia,Aly O. Abdelkareem,Islam Ahmed,Hesham Omran,Khaled Alashmouny,Christian Claudel,Abduallah Mohamed*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Mem++ 提出一种非破坏性记忆框架，从“写入时蒸馏”转向“读取时选择”，以支持组织决策随文档随时间演变场景下的时序问答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体参与组织工作，决策常以新文档而非编辑形式记录，回答问题时需知道某时间点生效的版本。但多数记忆系统在写入时压缩记录（蒸馏为事实、笔记或图边），提前固定了可回答内容，无法支持时序推理。

Method: Mem++ 完整存储每份文档及其日期与作者，写入时不调用生成模型；读取时仅检索日期不晚于问题所涉时间的文档，并融合词法与语义排序；不覆盖旧版本，而保留所有版本，由作答模型自行选择。

Result: 在组织基准 OrgMemBench 上，Mem++ 较最强记忆系统基线在两种作答模型下提升 8.0–13.1 分；使用 gpt-4.1-mini 时取得最佳总分，比 RAG 高 2.6 分。此外在 LoCoMo 上获最佳 LLM 评委均分，在 LongMemEval-S 上排名第二，仅次于其实体图变体。

Conclusion: 保留完整、带时间戳的文档并将选择推迟到读取时，优于写入时蒸馏，能更好支持组织场景下的时序问答。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents now take part in organizational work, where many authors record decisions across documents over months. Because a revised decision arrives as a new document rather than an edit, answering a question requires knowing which version held at a given time. However, most memory systems compress the record at write time. By distilling each document into facts, notes or graph edges, these methods fix what can be answered before any question is asked. To address this, we propose Mem++, a non-destructive memory framework shifting from write-time distillation to read-time selection. Mem++ stores every document whole with its date and author, and it calls no generative model at write time. At read time, it retrieves only documents dated up to the time a question asks about and fuses lexical and semantic rankings. Unlike systems that overwrite older versions, Mem++ keeps them and leaves the choice to the answering model. Evaluations on the organizational benchmark OrgMemBench demonstrate that Mem++ surpasses the strongest memory system baseline by 8.0 to 13.1 points across two answering models. With gpt-4.1-mini, it also achieves the best overall score, 2.6 points above RAG. In addition, Mem++ achieves the best average LLM-judge score on LoCoMo and ranks second on LongMemEval-S, behind only its entity-graph variant. Code for benchmark evaluation is available at https://github.com/AIDAChip-Inc/mem-plus-plus.

</details>


### [275] [Controllable Multi-label Video Safety Detection via Adaptive Tversky Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02019)
*Guangyu Yang,Jingbiao Mei,Mingsheng Sun,Jinghong Chen,Yingtong Bu,Pengda Qin,Da Chen,Bill Byrne*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出ATPO强化学习框架用于多标签视频安全检测，通过自适应Tversky奖励实现精确率-召回率可控调节，Jaccard指数大幅提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频社交媒体有害内容增长，需要自动化安全检测。现有VLM有害视频检测存在两个局限：一是将安全检测简化为二分类，忽略了不安全视频固有的多标签特性；二是依赖静态训练目标，无法支持可控的精确率-召回率权衡，而不同审核流程和不安全类别所需的操作点各不相同。

Method: 提出Adaptive Tversky Policy Optimization (ATPO)，一个面向多标签视频安全检测（Multi-VSD）的强化学习框架。核心是Adaptive Tversky Reward (ATR)，在训练过程中动态调整假阳性与假阴性惩罚，从而实现可控的精确率-召回率权衡。

Result: 在SafeWatch-Bench和XD-Violence上，ATPO显著提升多标签性能，SafeWatch-Bench-Real上的Jaccard指数从40.66提升至75.44。ATR能可靠地引导精确率-召回率操作点，支持异构策略需求的部署场景。代码与检查点已公开。

Conclusion: ATPO通过自适应奖励机制有效解决了多标签视频安全检测中静态目标的局限，实现了性能提升与精确率-召回率权衡的可控性，适用于多样化的内容审核部署需求。

Abstract: The rapid growth of video-based social media has increased users' exposure to harmful content, creating a need for reliable automated video safety detection. Although recent Vision-Language Models (VLMs) show strong video understanding capabilities, existing harmful video detection systems face two key limitations: they typically reduce safety detection to binary classification, overlooking the inherently multi-label nature of unsafe videos, and they rely on static training objectives that do not support controllable precision-recall trade-offs, though the desired operating point may vary across moderation pipelines and unsafe categories. To address these gaps, we propose Adaptive Tversky Policy Optimization (ATPO), a reinforcement learning framework for Multi-label Video Safety Detection (Multi-VSD). ATPO introduces the Adaptive Tversky Reward (ATR), which dynamically adjusts false-positive and false-negative penalties during training to enable controllable precision-recall trade-offs. Experiments on SafeWatch-Bench and XD-Violence show that ATPO substantially improves multi-label performance, increasing the Jaccard Index from 40.66 to 75.44 on SafeWatch-Bench-Real. Moreover, ATR enables reliable steering of the precision-recall operating point, supporting deployment scenarios with heterogeneous policy requirements. Code and checkpoints are provided at https://bruceyg.github.io/ATPO-project-page/ .

</details>


### [276] [Old Ideas, Novel Problems: The Instability of LLM-Based Novelty Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02022)
*Noy Sternlicht,Simra Shahid,Peter Jansen,Daniel S. Weld,Pao Siangliulue,Tom Hope*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了自动化创意系统中"新颖性评审器"（novelty judges）的设计选择问题。作者自动构建评测集，发现提示词的微小设计差异会导致评审结果剧变，而检索增强与更强推理能力帮助有限，专用新颖性评估器甚至不如最简单的提示基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化创意系统（automated ideation）常以生成想法的"新颖性"作为评价指标，而这一判断越来越多地交给大语言模型完成。然而这些"评审器"多是为特定任务临时搭建（ad hoc），即便做过验证，也往往是在人类撰写的论文而非其真正要打分的生成想法上进行。因此作者提出核心问题：这些新颖性评审器到底表现如何？

Method: 作者开展了一项系统的、受控的新颖性评估设计选择研究。首先自动构建评测集：从 OpenReview 中挖掘评审者明确肯定或质疑论文原创性的文本片段，仅保留在其研究领域极端两端获得一致同意（unanimous agreement）的投稿；再将这些与一个普通（vanilla）LLM 生成器产出的想法配对。随后在六种评审器上，系统变化提示设计、检索增强、推理预算等变量进行对比测试。

Result: 结果并不理想。提示设计的小改动会带来巨大后果：例如仅告知评审器"审稿人认为其中一个想法新颖、另一个不新颖"，就能使其对超过一半的相同想法对的判定发生改变，成对准确率（pairwise accuracy）波动超过 50 个百分点，有时甚至低于随机水平（chance）。同样的改动对一个评审器有益、对另一个有害。检索增强与更大的推理预算帮助甚微；两个专门构建（purpose-built）的新颖性评估器反而被作者最廉价的提示基线所超越。

Conclusion: 这些结果对自动化创意系统所报告的"新颖性提升"提出了质疑，并呼吁建立更稳健的新颖性评估方法。

Abstract: Automated ideation systems are often evaluated on the novelty of the ideas they produce, and that judgment is increasingly delegated to large language models. Such judges are typically built ad hoc and validated, if at all, on human-authored papers rather than on the generated ideas they are meant to score. So, how do novelty judges perform?
  Not well. We present a systematic controlled study of novelty evaluation design choices. We first build an evaluation set automatically, mining OpenReview for passages where reviewers explicitly affirm or dispute a paper's originality and keeping only submissions with unanimous agreement at the extremes of their research area; we pair these with ideas from a vanilla LLM generator. Across six judges, we find that small prompt design choices have large consequences; e.g., simply telling the judge that reviewers found one idea novel and the other not can change its verdict on more than half of the identical idea pairs it is shown, shifting pairwise accuracy by over 50 points and occasionally pushing it below chance. The same change helps one judge and hurts another. Retrieval and larger reasoning budgets help little, and two purpose-built novelty evaluators are outperformed by our cheapest prompted baseline. These results raise questions about reported novelty gains of automated ideation systems, and call for robust novelty evaluation methods.

</details>


### [277] [Typological Alignment of Stack-Based Language Models on Mildly Context-Sensitive Artificial Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02040)
*Nadine El-Naggar,Tatsuki Kuribayashi,Ted Briscoe*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文从数据和模型两侧扩展了语言模型学习偏差与语言类型共性关系的分析，聚焦于交叉序列依存和栈式语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言中某些属性（如SOV语序）在数千种已记录语言中更为普遍，这种类型学共性常被归因于学习偏差。计算模拟（近期使用语言模型）为探索该理论提供了便利，本文旨在进一步扩展相关分析。

Method: 在数据侧聚焦交叉序列依存（已记录句法复杂度的上限），在模型侧聚焦栈式语言模型（SLM），评估其在多种人工语言上对交叉序列依存的泛化能力。

Result: 实验确认栈式语言模型难以处理交叉序列依存结构；但工作记忆受限的栈式语言模型泛化能力更好。

Conclusion: 工作记忆受限带来的归纳偏差可能是某些语序配置类型学共性的潜在基础，为学习偏差理论提供了计算证据。

Abstract: Some properties of languages, e.g., subject-object-verb (SOV) word order, are more prevalent than others among the thousands of attested natural languages (NLs). Such typological commonality is often attributed to learning biases. Computational simulations, recently with language models (LMs), have facilitated the exploration of this theory. In this paper, we extend existing analyses of the relationship between LMs' learning biases and typological commonality on both data and model sides, focusing on: (i) cross-serial dependencies, the upper limit of attested syntactic complexity, and (ii) stack-based LMs (SLMs), potentially facilitating learning of hierarchical patterns. We first evaluate generalization of SLMs on cross-serial dependencies across diverse artificial languages and confirm that they struggle with such constructions. However, SLMs with limited working memory generalize better suggesting a possible basis for such inductive bias and thus the typological commonality of some word order configurations.

</details>


### [278] [LLM2Jev: LLMs Are Already Jev-Style Decision Models -- When and How to Fine-Tune Them](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02076)
*Yinheng Li,Justin Wagle*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 LLM2Jev，一种保持架构不变的框架，直接从 LLM 下一 token 概率中提取校准的分类决策，并研究通用 LLM 何时开箱即用、何时需要微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Jev 式决策模型能直接输出类别概率分布供软件系统使用，但需专门训练。作者希望探究通用 LLM 是否已具备该能力，以及微调在何种情况下才真正必要。

Method: LLM2Jev 通过带括号的数字标识符上的下一 token 概率提取校准决策，提供免训练推理流程，以及一种微调目标：用树因子化的 listwise 损失优化候选选择，并用 KL 散度惩罚将辅助预测锚定到基础模型。

Result: 在 Qwen3.5-4B 和 Qwen3-0.6B 上评估发现：免训练时 4B 模型可媲美同骨干的社区 Jev 模型，优于字母 logit 读取，支持任意选项数量，并原生处理图像多模态决策；微调带来针对性而非普遍性收益——显著提升弱模型和特定任务（如多选项意图路由），但对强骨干收益递减；KL 锚定可防止对话文本生成能力退化，LoRA 在强模型上表现最佳。

Conclusion: 现代 LLM 本身即是有效的决策模型，微调仅在特定场景必要；LLM2Jev 在保持架构与生成能力的同时实现了校准决策输出。

Abstract: Jev-style decision models return categorical probability distributions over predefined options without generating free-form text, enabling software systems to act on their outputs directly. In this work, we investigate the extent to which general-purpose LLMs already possess this capability out of the box, and when fine-tuning is actually necessary. We present LLM2Jev, an architecture-preserving framework that extracts calibrated decisions directly from next-token probabilities over bracketed numeric identifiers. LLM2Jev provides both a training-free inference recipe and a fine-tuning objective that optimizes candidate selection via a tree-factorized listwise loss while anchoring auxiliary predictions to the base model using KL divergence penalties. Evaluating on Qwen3.5-4B and Qwen3-0.6B, we find that modern LLMs are inherently effective decision models: without training, the 4B model matches community Jev-style models built on the same backbone, outperforms letter-logit readouts, supports arbitrary option counts, and natively handles multimodal decisions over images. Fine-tuning provides targeted rather than universal benefits -- substantially improving weaker models and specific tasks (such as many-option intent routing), but offering diminishing returns for strong backbones. Crucially, our KL anchors prevent behavioral degradation in conversational text generation, with LoRA delivering the strongest performance on capable models.

</details>


### [279] [Scalable, Transferable Meta-network for Data Selection Requires a Different Loss (and Why the Obvious Choice is Problematic)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02092)
*Zilin Du,Bowen Yang,Boyang Albert Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出TESS框架，用点式价值匹配目标训练选择网络，实现可迁移的大规模语言模型训练数据选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模异质语料上训练大语言模型时，数据选择至关重要。元学习式训练数据选择（MTS）比启发式打分更有原则性，但现有方法在细粒度估值与对未见数据的可迁移性之间存在权衡。用选择网络替代逐样本权重是自然的解决思路，但直接将其嵌入现有MTS目标会导致权重抑制与对易学特征的持续依赖，从而引发优化不稳定、泛化差的问题。

Method: 提出Transferable Example Scoring and Selection（TESS），一个可扩展的数据选择框架，构建在点式价值匹配目标（Pointwise Value Matching, PVM）之上，替代原有MTS目标以缓解权重抑制与易学特征依赖问题。

Result: 在LLM安全性和定向指令微调任务上，TESS展现出强迁移性：从子集到全语料、从小模型到大模型均可有效迁移。

Conclusion: TESS通过PVM目标有效解决了选择网络在MTS中的优化不稳定与迁移性差的问题，为大规模语言模型的数据选择提供了可迁移、可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Data selection is critical for training large language models on massive and heterogeneous corpora. Meta-learning for Training-data Selection offers a principled alternative to heuristic scoring by learning data weights from a target validation objective, but existing methods face a trade-off between fine-grained valuation and transferability to unseen data. A natural solution is to replace per-sample weights with a selection network. However, we find that directly incorporating such a network into existing MTS objectives leads to unstable optimization and poor generalization, caused by weight suppression and persistent reliance on easy-to-learn features. To address these issues, we propose Transferable Example Scoring and Selection (TESS), a scalable data-selection framework built on a Pointwise Value Matching objective (PVM). Experiments on LLM safety and targeted instruction tuning demonstrate strong transfer across datasets, from subsets to full corpora, and from smaller to larger models.

</details>


### [280] [Argo-Bench: Evaluating Data Agents on Enterprise-Scale Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02122)
*Gabriel Tomitsuka,Arman Raayatsanati,Emma Xing,Duke Gand,Joseph J Ma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Argo-Bench 是一个面向企业级数据科学与分析任务的评测框架，包含 210 个任务，模拟纽约市外卖平台的真实仓库环境，要求智能体在多表仓库中重建事实并执行行动，用模拟器后果评分，超越传统 text-to-SQL 评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实企业数据分析需要在数十张表间推理、执行统计分析并采取行动。现有 text-to-SQL 基准只评估查询生成，答案键常有错误；且因企业仓库敏感，基准多建立在公共单表单事件数据集上，无法反映真实复杂度。因此需要更贴近真实企业环境且能评估"行动后果"的基准。

Method: 构建 Argo-Bench：包含 210 个数据科学与分析任务。基于公共数据、行业文献和监管文件，模拟一个纽约市外卖平台（2024 年 8100 万订单），具备真实经济、欺诈模式和市场激励机制；导出为仿 Oracle E-Business Suite 的 235 张表、75 亿行的 ERP 仓库。模拟器真值状态对智能体隐藏，任务须先导航仓库重建事实再行动。智能体需执行封禁欺诈账户、分配激励预算、发放补发工资等动作，由评分器按模拟器中的后果打分。每个任务都有可执行参考解。

Result: 14 个前沿与开源权重模型中最强者仅在 34.8% 的任务上达到 95 分及以上，平均得分 59.5 分。

Conclusion: Argo-Bench 将评测从 text-to-SQL 拓展到"理解、导航并作用于真实数据环境"的智能体，期望推动该方向进展。

Abstract: Real-world enterprise data science and analytics workflows require reasoning across dozens of tables, performing statistical analyses, and acting on the results. Established text-to-SQL benchmarks evaluate query generation alone, and audits have found their answer keys frequently wrong. Because real enterprise warehouses are too sensitive to release, these benchmarks are built on public datasets where a business event fits in a single table. We introduce Argo-Bench, an evaluation framework comprising 210 data science and analytics tasks. Drawing on public data, peer-reviewed industry literature, and regulatory filings, we simulate a food delivery platform in New York City at true scale, with 81 million orders in 2024, grounded economics, fraud patterns, and marketplace incentives. We export this world to an ERP warehouse of 235 tables and 7.5 billion rows, modeled on the Oracle E-Business Suite schema. The simulator's ground-truth state is withheld from the warehouse the agent sees, so tasks require reconstructing facts by navigating the warehouse before acting on them. Argo-Bench goes beyond text-to-SQL: the agent files actions such as banning fraudulent accounts, allocating courier incentive budgets, or issuing back pay, and the grader scores each by its consequences in the simulator. Every task has an executable reference solution that demonstrates solvability using only the warehouse. The strongest of 14 frontier and open-weight models scores 95 or higher on only 34.8% of tasks and averages 59.5 points. We hope Argo-Bench drives progress toward agents that understand, navigate, and act within real data environments.

</details>


### [281] [Keyword Harnesses Fail Open: A Cheap Diagnostic Ladder for Tool-Use Claims in Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02142)
*Juan S. Santillana*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文揭示了工具使用基准中关键词匹配导致的假阳性问题：一个1B模型仅因关键词匹配得分与600M模型相当，但实际无法生成有效工具调用；作者提出一套廉价诊断方法定位并修复该缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具使用基准多依赖关键词匹配，可能给从未真正执行工具调用的小模型错误加分（假阳性）。作者在某对匹配架构的西班牙语安全模型中发现这一现象，希望提供严格且低成本的诊断手段来验证和修复。

Method: 对比一对共享解码器、分词器和特殊token的模型（661.6M参数、约65%代码/技术文本、无专用SFT；1,109M参数、网络密集型多阶段课程、6B token工具SFT）。采用逐字复现检查、首token探针、针对性SFT修复（多样语料、5倍学习率、2202步、约3.3 GPU小时）、嵌入漂移检查和因子分析等阶梯式诊断。

Result: 宽松指标下两模型几乎相同（B4: 0.660 vs 0.650）；逐字复现中600M在6/6样例生成有效调用，1B在0/6。首token探针将1B失败定位到被网络密集型训练阶段抹除的先验（<|tool_call|>概率10^-4~10^-5）。修复后1B在269行语料上有效率从0.100升至0.959（600M为0.926），在238个未见提示上达0.536优于600M的0.428（p=0.004），且97.7%的bf16嵌入表保持位级一致。两模型均存在过度触发问题（负面提示下不调用比例0.09和0.17）。

Conclusion: 关键词匹配基准会掩盖小模型工具使用的真实缺陷；提出的廉价诊断阶梯仅需数分钟CPU时间，能有效定位并修复问题，应作为小模型工具使用声明的门槛。多样语料对抑制过度触发的益处仍属假设，受种子敏感性影响。

Abstract: Keyword-matching benchmarks can credit small models for tool use they never perform. We document such a false positive in a matched-architecture pair of Spanish security language models and propose a ladder of strict, cheap diagnostics. A 661.6M parameter model (approx. 65% code/technical text; no dedicated SFT) and a 1,109M model (web-heavy multi-phase curriculum; 6B-token tool-SFT) share decoder, tokenizer, and special tokens, scoring almost identically on lenient tool-use metrics (B4: 0.660 vs. 0.650).
  Verbatim-reproduction checks on training examples separate them completely: the 600M emits valid tool calls with generalized arguments on 6/6 examples; the 1B does so on 0/6 across checkpoints. A first-token probe localizes the 1B's failure to a missing prior (prob. $10^{-4}$--$10^{-5}$ on <|tool_call|>), which was erased by its web-heavy training phase. A targeted SFT recipe (diverse corpus, 5x higher learning rate, 2,202 steps, ~3.3 GPU-hours) repairs the 1B using three orders of magnitude fewer tokens than the failed phase. On all 269 corpus rows, valid emission rises from 0.100 to 0.959 (600M: 0.926). On 238 unseen prompts, the repaired 1B passes 0.536 vs. the 600M's 0.428 ($p = 0.004$).
