Embedding-drift checks show the repair did not move the trigger token's tied embedding (97.7% of the bf16 table remains bit-identical), meaning changes live in the surrounding network.
  Both models over-trigger, rarely answering negative prompts without a call (0.09 for 600M, 0.17 for repaired 1B). Factorial analyses confirm all repair configurations install the format, though suppression benefits from a diverse corpus remain a hypothesis due to seed sensitivity. This cheap diagnostic ladder costs minutes of CPU time and should gate tool-use claims on small models.

</details>


### [282] [From Knowledge Access to Source Learning: Developing Source-Specific Competence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02150)
*Lucheng Fu,Kejing Xia,Yiyang Wang,Yiqiao Jin,Jinjin He,Xiyuan Yang,Haoxin Liu,Ye Yu,Haibo Jin,Yijia Xiao,Wenke Lee,B. Aditya Prakash,Haohan Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究"源学习"（source learning），提出 SourceLearn，通过自导源学习与任务引导源学习两种机制，为持久权威源构建可复用的"源模型"，在五个基准、三个 LLM 后端上 15 个设置中 13 个取得最佳表现，最高超过 Hybrid RAG 22.6 分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体在解决连续的知识密集任务时依赖持久外部源。现有方法侧重改进源内容的访问与组织，智能体记忆系统保留可复用知识，但同一源的重复使用多被视为"重复访问"，而非逐步深化对该源理解的机会。因此作者提出研究"源学习"，即围绕持久权威源发展可复用的源特定能力。

Method: 用"持久源模型"表征这种能力，捕获对源的可复用理解（知识如何被结构化、解释与应用）。提出 SourceLearn，结合两种互补学习机制：自导源学习（Self-Directed Source Learning）识别尚未充分理解之处并自适应回访源；任务引导源学习（Task-Guided Source Learning）利用下游经验揭示局部表征缺口与知识组织的反复需求。两者中，学习信号决定应重新考虑什么，持久更新则从权威源重建。

Result: 在五个基准、三个 LLM 后端上，SourceLearn 在 15 个设置中的 13 个取得最佳性能，较 Hybrid RAG 提升最高达 22.6 分，相较静态源表征与基于经验的记忆基线也有显著整体提升。

Conclusion: 将同一源的重复使用视为逐步深化理解的机会而非单纯重复访问是有效的。SourceLearn 通过双机制持续构建与精炼源模型，能够稳定优于静态源表征、经验记忆与 RAG 类基线，验证了源学习范式的价值。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly rely on persistent external sources to solve sequences of knowledge-intensive tasks. Existing methods improve how source content is accessed and organized, while agent-memory systems preserve reusable knowledge from prior interactions, but repeated use of the same source is still largely treated as repeated access rather than an opportunity to progressively improve understanding of that source. We study source learning: developing reusable source-specific competence over a persistent authoritative source. We represent this competence with a persistent source model that captures reusable understanding of the source, including how its knowledge is structured, interpreted, and applied. To construct and progressively refine such models, we propose SourceLearn, which combines two complementary learning mechanisms. Self-Directed Source Learning identifies what remains incompletely understood and adaptively revisits the source, while Task-Guided Source Learning uses downstream experience to reveal local representational gaps and recurring needs in how source knowledge should be organized. In both cases, learning signals determine what should be reconsidered, while persistent updates are reconstructed from the authoritative source. Across five benchmarks and three LLM backends, SourceLearn achieves the best performance in 13 of 15 settings, with gains of up to 22.6 points over Hybrid RAG and substantial overall improvements over static source representations and experience-based memory baselines.

</details>


### [283] [AutoCompact: Learning When to Compact Context in Long-Horizon Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02163)
*Xuan Zhang,Longtao Zheng,Cunxiao Du,Bo An,Xin Dong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AutoCompact 让编程智能体把"何时压缩上下文、保留哪些工作状态、如何续接"纳入策略学习，通过裁判纠错构造轨迹并做 SFT + RL，在 SWE-bench Verified 与 SWE-PolyBench Verified 上分别绝对提升 9.2% 和 5.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仓库级软件工程任务需要长轨迹的代码检查、搜索、编辑与测试。随着任务推进，早期探索逐渐失效，因此上下文管理不只是避免溢出，更关键的是决定何时压缩、保留哪些工作状态以及如何从中继续。现有方法未把这类决策作为策略的一部分来学习。

Method: 先让基础智能体在编程任务上运行，用裁判审查其压缩决策、摘要及压缩后的动作；将有缺陷的输出替换为修正版本后再在环境中执行，使轨迹从修正后的决策继续。用这些轨迹做监督微调，随后通过带任务成功奖励的强化学习联合优化编程与压缩能力。

Result: 在 SWE-bench Verified 和 SWE-PolyBench Verified 上，AutoCompact 相比基础模型的通过率分别绝对提升 9.2% 与 5.0%。改进在所有评估的推理预算下均成立，包括从不溢出的 256K 上下文窗口，以及溢出会触发回退压缩的 16K 窗口。

Conclusion: 把上下文压缩决策内化为智能体策略的一部分，可有效提升仓库级编程任务的长期表现，且在不同上下文预算下具有稳健性。

Abstract: Coding agents solve repository-level software engineering tasks through long trajectories of code inspection, search, editing, and testing. As a task progresses, earlier exploration becomes stale, so managing context is more than avoiding overflow: an agent must decide when to compact, what working state to preserve, and how to continue from it. We introduce AutoCompact, which trains a coding agent to make these decisions as part of its policy. To collect training data, we run the base agent on coding tasks and use a judge to review its compaction decisions, summaries, and actions after compaction. Flawed outputs are replaced with corrected ones before being executed in the environment, so each trajectory continues from the corrected decisions. We use these trajectories for supervised fine-tuning, then jointly optimize coding and compaction through reinforcement learning with task-success rewards. Experiments on SWE-bench Verified and SWE-PolyBench Verified show that AutoCompact improves pass rates over the base model by an absolute 9.2\% and 5.0\%, respectively. The improvements hold across all evaluated inference budgets, with a 256K context window that never overflows and with a 16K window whose overflow triggers fallback compaction.

</details>


### [284] [Hierarchical Continuous Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02193)
*Hui Ren,Zihan Li,Chang Liu,Huidong Liu,Alexander Schwing*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出HC-DLM，将离散词元生成与连续隐轨迹耦合进统一的去噪过程，训练目标源自词元似然的变分下界，在Sudoku、Countdown和LM1B上超越同规模离散与连续扩散基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散语言模型并行解码时词元独立采样，破坏了同一批解码词元间的统计依赖；连续扩散模型虽用共享连续状态去噪，但去噪器仅看到该状态，在最终解码前与有效词元配置无约束关联。

Method: 提出分层连续扩散语言模型（HC-DLM）：在单一、有原则的去噪过程中耦合离散词元生成与连续隐轨迹，训练目标由词元似然的变分上界（下界）导出；与将连续上下文附加到自包含离散链的近期方法不同，HC-DLM使隐状态成为唯一持久的生成状态，每一步从中读出词元，再作为下一隐更新的支架反馈。

Result: 在结构化推理（Sudoku）、数学规划（Countdown）和语言建模（LM1B）上，相同模型规模下HC-DLM优于离散与连续扩散基线，体现在Sudoku和Countdown的谜题准确率及LM1B的生成困惑度上。

Conclusion: HC-DLM通过以连续隐轨迹为唯一持久生成状态并循环读出/反馈词元，缓解了并行解码中统计依赖缺失的问题，验证了分层连续扩散在结构化推理与语言建模上的有效性。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion language models offer a compelling alternative to autoregressive generation for tasks demanding bidirectional reasoning and global constraint satisfaction. Yet they share a structural bottleneck: when decoding in parallel, each token is sampled independently from its marginal, severing the statistical dependencies among the tokens decoded together. Continuous diffusion language models avoid this by denoising a shared continuous state, but their denoiser sees only that state, so nothing ties it to a valid token configuration until it is finally decoded. To address this, we propose Hierarchical Continuous Diffusion Language Models (HC-DLM), which couple discrete token generation with a continuous latent trajectory in a single, principled denoising process, whose training objective is derived from a variational bound on the token likelihood. In contrast to recent methods that attach continuous context to a self-contained discrete chain, HC-DLM makes the latent the only persistent generative state: tokens are read out from it at every step and feed back as a scaffold for the next latent update. On structured reasoning (Sudoku), mathematical planning (Countdown) and language modeling (LM1B), HC-DLM improves over discrete and continuous diffusion baselines at matched model size, in puzzle accuracy on Sudoku and Countdown and in generative perplexity on LM1B. Project page: https://hc-dlm.github.io/.

</details>


### [285] [KaliBench: A Fine-Grained Benchmark for Cybersecurity Tool Use on Kali Linux with Runtime-Free Verifiable Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02206)
*Pengfei Li,Naufal Suryanto,Sicheng Zhang,Muzammal Naseer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 KaliBench，一个面向 Kali Linux 的自然语言到命令行（CLI）翻译的细粒度基准与数据集，包含 8,504 条查询-命令对，覆盖 1,642 个工具，用于评测和训练 LLM 在网络安全工具使用中的可执行命令生成能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对 LLM 网络安全能力的评测多集中于知识问答或端到端智能体任务，未直接衡量模型为真实网络安全工具生成可执行命令的能力。而网络安全操作依赖严格的命令行接口，微小的语法错误、标志-取值绑定错误或参数顺序错误都会导致执行失败，因此该评测缺口十分关键。

Method: KaliBench 通过基于文档（manuscript-grounded）的构建流程生成，采用确定性规范化与别名感知评测；并设计多阶段验证流程，结合 LLM 校验、沙箱终端执行与人在环精修，以保证语义正确性与实际可执行性；同时基于细粒度确定性信号支持免运行时（runtime-free）的可验证奖励用于训练。

Result: 数据集共 8,504 条查询-命令对，覆盖 1,642 个工具、23 个能力维度和 5 个安全阶段。在三种评测模式和 24 种通用及安全专用开源权重模型配置下，无限制设置中没有任何开源权重模型的精确命令准确率超过 42%；使用 KaliBench 派生的可验证奖励进行监督微调与强化学习，可显著提升 8B 模型，性能媲美 685B MoE 模型。

Conclusion: KaliBench 揭示了 LLM 在无工具提示下准确使用 CLI 网络安全工具的显著困难，并证明其细粒度、确定性、可验证的信号既能用于严谨可复现的评测，也能有效驱动模型训练与能力提升。

Abstract: LLMs are increasingly applied to cybersecurity workflows, where they are expected to translate analysts' intent into tool invocations. However, existing evaluations focus on knowledge-based assessments or end-to-end agentic tasks, and do not directly measure LLMs' ability to generate executable commands for real-world cybersecurity tools. This gap is critical because cybersecurity operations rely on strict command-line interfaces (CLIs), where minor syntax errors, incorrect flag--value bindings, or argument misordering can invalidate execution. We introduce KaliBench, a fine-grained benchmark and dataset for natural-language--to--CLI translation on Kali Linux, comprising 8,504 query--command pairs spanning 1,642 tools across 23 capability dimensions and 5 security phases. KaliBench is constructed via a manuscript-grounded pipeline with deterministic canonicalization and alias-aware evaluation, enabling precise and reproducible assessment of tool selection and argument construction. To ensure both semantic correctness and practical executability, we develop a multi-stage verification pipeline that combines LLM-based validation, sandboxed terminal execution, and human-in-the-loop refinement. Building on these fine-grained, deterministic signals, KaliBench further enables runtime-free verifiable rewards for training. Across three evaluation modes and 24 configurations of general-purpose and security-focused open-weight models, no open-weight model exceeds 42% exact-command accuracy in the unrestricted setting, highlighting the difficulty of accurate CLI-based cybersecurity tool use without explicit tool hints. We further show that supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards derived from KaliBench significantly improve an 8B model and achieve performance comparable to a 685B MoE model.

</details>
